===== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/vnchflex/vietkeyboard/ime/ClipboardStore.kt =====
package com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.ime

import android.content.Context
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject

data class ClipboardItem(
    val id: String,
    val text: String,
    val timestamp: Long,
    val isPinned: Boolean = false
)

/**
 * Persistent Gboard-style clipboard history store.
 * - Unpinned items expire automatically after 1 hour (3,600,000 ms).
 * - Pinned items are kept indefinitely and sorted before unpinned items.
 */
class ClipboardStore(context: Context) {
    private val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences("vnchflex_clipboard", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    companion object {
        private const val KEY_ITEMS = "clipboard_items_json"
        const val UNPINNED_TTL_MS = 60L * 60L * 1000L // 1 hour
        const val MAX_ITEMS = 20
    }

    fun getItems(now: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()): List<ClipboardItem> {
        val raw = loadRaw()
        val valid = raw.filter { it.isPinned || (now - it.timestamp) <= UNPINNED_TTL_MS }
        if (valid.size != raw.size) {
            saveRaw(valid)
        }
        return valid.sortedWith(
            compareByDescending<ClipboardItem> { it.isPinned }
                .thenByDescending { it.timestamp }
        )
    }

    fun addClip(text: String, now: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()) {
        val trimmed = text
        if (trimmed.isBlank()) return
        val current = loadRaw().filter { it.isPinned || (now - it.timestamp) <= UNPINNED_TTL_MS }.toMutableList()
        val existingIdx = current.indexOfFirst { it.text == trimmed }
        if (existingIdx >= 0) {
            val existing = current.removeAt(existingIdx)
            current.add(0, existing.copy(timestamp = now))
        } else {
            val id = "${now}_${trimmed.hashCode()}"
            current.add(0, ClipboardItem(id = id, text = trimmed, timestamp = now, isPinned = false))
        }
        // Keep pinned items + most recent unpinned up to MAX_ITEMS
        val pinned = current.filter { it.isPinned }
        val unpinned = current.filter { !it.isPinned }.take((MAX_ITEMS - pinned.size).coerceAtLeast(0))
        saveRaw(pinned + unpinned)
    }

    fun togglePin(id: String) {
        val current = loadRaw().map {
            if (it.id == id) it.copy(isPinned = !it.isPinned) else it
        }
        saveRaw(current)
    }

    fun delete(id: String) {
        val current = loadRaw().filterNot { it.id == id }
        saveRaw(current)
    }

    private fun loadRaw(): List<ClipboardItem> {
        val json = prefs.getString(KEY_ITEMS, null) ?: return emptyList()
        return try {
            val arr = JSONArray(json)
            buildList(arr.length()) {
                for (i in 0 until arr.length()) {
                    val obj = arr.getJSONObject(i)
                    add(
                        ClipboardItem(
                            id = obj.getString("id"),
                            text = obj.getString("text"),
                            timestamp = obj.getLong("timestamp"),
                            isPinned = obj.optBoolean("isPinned", false)
                        )
                    )
                }
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            emptyList()
        }
    }

    private fun saveRaw(items: List<ClipboardItem>) {
        val arr = JSONArray()
        for (item in items) {
            arr.put(
                JSONObject().apply {
                    put("id", item.id)
                    put("text", item.text)
                    put("timestamp", item.timestamp)
                    put("isPinned", item.isPinned)
                }
            )
        }
        prefs.edit().putString(KEY_ITEMS, arr.toString()).apply()
    }
}
===== END FILE: app/src/main/java/com/vnchflex/vietkeyboard/ime/ClipboardStore.kt =====

===== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/vnchflex/vietkeyboard/ime/GlideDecoder.kt =====
package com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.ime

import com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.engine.DiacriticUtils
import com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.suggestion.SuggestionEngine

/**
 * Lightweight Vietnamese + English Glide Typing Decoder:
 * - Normalizes consecutive duplicate letters (`collapseRepeats`: `"letter"` -> `"leter"`, `"book"` -> `"bok"`)
 *   so words with double letters get the full subsequence bonus and zero edit-distance penalty when swiped.
 * - Tolerates start/end key keyboard-neighbor misses (`areKeysAdjacent`) with a slight penalty instead of hard-dropping.
 * - Combines geometric path similarity, unigram frequency weight, and active Trigram `(prevWord2, prevWord1)` context boosts.
 */
object GlideDecoder {
    internal val KEY_NEIGHBORS: Map<Char, String> = mapOf(
        'q' to "wa", 'w' to "qeas", 'e' to "wrsd", 'r' to "etdf", 't' to "ryfg",
        'y' to "tugh", 'u' to "yihj", 'i' to "uojk", 'o' to "ipkl", 'p' to "ol",
        'a' to "qwsz", 's' to "weadzx", 'd' to "ersfxc", 'f' to "rtgvcd", 'g' to "tyhbvf",
        'h' to "yujnbg", 'j' to "uikmnh", 'k' to "AEiojl,m".lowercase(), 'l' to "opk",
        'z' to "asx", 'x' to "zsdc", 'c' to "xdfv", 'v' to "cfgb", 'b' to "vghn",
        'n' to "bhjm", 'm' to "njk"
    )

    private val KEY_POSITIONS: Map<Char, Pair<Float, Float>> = mapOf(
        'q' to (0.05f to 0.16f), 'w' to (0.15f to 0.16f), 'e' to (0.25f to 0.16f), 'r' to (0.35f to 0.16f), 't' to (0.45f to 0.16f),
        'y' to (0.55f to 0.16f), 'u' to (0.65f to 0.16f), 'i' to (0.75f to 0.16f), 'o' to (0.85f to 0.16f), 'p' to (0.95f to 0.16f),
        'a' to (0.10f to 0.50f), 's' to (0.20f to 0.50f), 'd' to (0.30f to 0.50f), 'f' to (0.40f to 0.50f), 'g' to (0.50f to 0.50f),
        'h' to (0.60f to 0.50f), 'j' to (0.70f to 0.50f), 'k' to (0.80f to 0.50f), 'l' to (0.90f to 0.50f),
        'z' to (0.20f to 0.84f), 'x' to (0.30f to 0.84f), 'c' to (0.40f to 0.84f), 'v' to (0.50f to 0.84f), 'b' to (0.60f to 0.84f),
        'n' to (0.70f to 0.84f), 'm' to (0.80f to 0.84f)
    )

    private fun dtwDistance(pathA: List<Pair<Float, Float>>, pathB: List<Pair<Float, Float>>): Float {
        if (pathA.isEmpty() || pathB.isEmpty()) return 0f
        val n = pathA.size
        val m = pathB.size
        val dp = Array(n + 1) { FloatArray(m + 1) { Float.POSITIVE_INFINITY } }
        dp[0][0] = 0f
        for (i in 1..n) {
            for (j in 1..m) {
                val dx = pathA[i - 1].first - pathB[j - 1].first
                val dy = pathA[i - 1].second - pathB[j - 1].second
                val cost = kotlin.math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
                dp[i][j] = cost + minOf(dp[i - 1][j], dp[i][j - 1], dp[i - 1][j - 1])
            }
        }
        return dp[n][m] / maxOf(n, m)
    }

    fun decode(
        swipedKeys: String,
        suggestionEngine: SuggestionEngine,
        prevWord1: String,
        prevWord2: String,
        limit: Int = 3,
        isVietnameseMode: Boolean = true
    ): List<String> {
        val raw = collapseRepeats(swipedKeys.lowercase())
        if (raw.length < 2) return emptyList()

        val swipePath = raw.mapNotNull { KEY_POSITIONS[it] }
        val firstChar = raw.first()
        val lastChar = raw.last()
        val vocab = suggestionEngine.getGlideVocabulary(isVietnameseMode)
        val contextWeights = suggestionEngine.getContextBoosts(prevWord2, prevWord1)

        val scored = mutableListOf<Pair<String, Int>>()
        for ((word, unigramWeight) in vocab) {
            val stripped = DiacriticUtils.strip(word).lowercase()
            if (stripped.length < 2) continue

            val collapsedTarget = collapseRepeats(stripped)
            val exactFirst = collapsedTarget.first() == firstChar
            val exactLast = collapsedTarget.last() == lastChar
            val nearFirst = exactFirst || isNeighbor(collapsedTarget.first(), firstChar)
            val nearLast = exactLast || isNeighbor(collapsedTarget.last(), lastChar)
            if (!nearFirst || !nearLast) continue

            val subseqMatched = isSubsequence(collapsedTarget, raw)
            val dist = levenshtein(collapsedTarget, raw)
            if (!subseqMatched && dist > (raw.length / 2).coerceAtLeast(2)) continue

            var score = unigramWeight
            if (exactFirst) score += 90 else score += 25
            if (exactLast) score += 90 else score += 25
            if (subseqMatched) score += 220
            if (dist == 0) score += 140
            val targetPath = collapsedTarget.mapNotNull { KEY_POSITIONS[it] }
            val dtw = dtwDistance(targetPath, swipePath)
            score -= (dtw * 180f).toInt()
            score -= dist * 45
            score -= kotlin.math.abs(collapsedTarget.length - raw.length) * 18
            score += (contextWeights[word] ?: 0) * 65
            scored.add(word to score)
        }

        return scored
            .sortedByDescending { it.second }
            .map { it.first }
            .distinct()
            .take(limit)
    }

    private fun isNeighbor(a: Char, b: Char): Boolean =
        a == b || KEY_NEIGHBORS[a]?.contains(b) == true

    private fun collapseRepeats(s: String): String {
        if (s.length <= 1) return s
        val sb = StringBuilder(s.length)
        var prev = '\u0000'
        for (c in s) {
            if (c != prev) {
                sb.append(c)
                prev = c
            }
        }
        return sb.toString()
    }

    private fun isSubsequence(target: String, source: String): Boolean {
        var ti = 0
        var si = 0
        while (ti < target.length && si < source.length) {
            if (target[ti] == source[si]) ti++
            si++
        }
        return ti == target.length
    }

    private fun levenshtein(a: String, b: String): Int {
        val m = a.length
        val n = b.length
        val dp = IntArray(n + 1) { it }
        for (i in 1..m) {
            var prev = dp[0]
            dp[0] = i
            for (j in 1..n) {
                val temp = dp[j]
                dp[j] = if (a[i - 1] == b[j - 1]) prev
                else 1 + minOf(prev, dp[j], dp[j - 1])
                prev = temp
            }
        }
        return dp[n]
    }
}

===== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/vnchflex/vietkeyboard/ime/KeyboardCanvasView.kt =====
package com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.ime

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.graphics.Path
import android.graphics.RectF
import android.graphics.Typeface
import android.graphics.drawable.Drawable
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import android.util.AttributeSet
import android.util.SparseArray
import android.view.MotionEvent
import android.view.View
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.R

/**
 * Gboard-architecture Single-Canvas Keyboard Renderer & Multitouch PointerDispatcher:
 * - Renders all keys (Letters, Number Row, Symbols 1 & 2, Shift, Backspace, Space, Enter) on a single Canvas
 *   with zero nested ViewGroup layout passes and zero per-frame allocations.
 * - Tracks each finger independently via [SparseArray] of [PointerTracker] (`ACTION_DOWN`, `ACTION_POINTER_DOWN`,
 *   `ACTION_MOVE`, `ACTION_UP`, `ACTION_POINTER_UP`) for true 2-thumb rollover.
 * - Integrates Glide Typing trail, Spacebar cursor control, and Backspace slide-to-select gesture delete
 *   in one unified touch pipeline.
 */
class KeyboardCanvasView @JvmOverloads constructor(
    context: Context,
    attrs: AttributeSet? = null
) : View(context, attrs) {

    enum class Page { LETTERS, SYMBOLS1, SYMBOLS2 }
    enum class KeyRole { CHAR, NUMBER, SHIFT, BACKSPACE, MODE_SYMBOLS1, MODE_SYMBOLS2, MODE_LETTERS, COMMA, DOT, SWITCH_LANG, SPACE, ENTER }

    data class KeySpec(
        val id: String,
        val role: KeyRole,
        val primaryLabel: String,
        val secondaryLabel: String? = null,
        val weight: Float = 1f,
        val rect: RectF = RectF(),
        val hitRect: RectF = RectF()
    )

    private class PointerTracker(
        val pointerId: Int,
        var downKey: KeySpec?,
        var currentKey: KeySpec?,
        val startX: Float,
        val startY: Float,
        var lastX: Float,
        var lastY: Float,
        var committedOnDown: Boolean = false,
        var longPressTriggered: Boolean = false,
        var isSpaceSwiping: Boolean = false,
        var isBackspaceSwiping: Boolean = false,
        var longPressRunnable: Runnable? = null
    )

    // Callbacks to VietKeyboardService
    var onLetterDown: ((Char) -> Unit)? = null
    var onDigitOrSymbol: ((String) -> Unit)? = null
    var onSecondaryCommit: ((String) -> Unit)? = null
    var onUndoDownLetter: (() -> Unit)? = null
    var onShiftClick: (() -> Unit)? = null
    var onBackspaceDown: (() -> Unit)? = null
    var onBackspaceRepeatEnd: (() -> Unit)? = null
    var onBackspaceGestureSelect: ((wordsCount: Int, isFirstTrigger: Boolean) -> Unit)? = null
    var onBackspaceGestureFinish: ((wordsCount: Int, cancelled: Boolean) -> Unit)? = null
    var onSpaceDown: (() -> Unit)? = null
    var onUndoDownSpace: (() -> Unit)? = null
    var onSpaceCursorMove: ((delta: Int) -> Unit)? = null
    var onSpaceLongPress: (() -> Unit)? = null
    var onCommaDown: (() -> Unit)? = null
    var onCommaLongPress: ((anchorRect: RectF) -> Unit)? = null
    var onDotDown: (() -> Unit)? = null
    var onUndoDownPunctuation: ((Char) -> Unit)? = null
    var onSwitchLanguage: (() -> Unit)? = null
    var onEnterClick: (() -> Unit)? = null
    var onGlideStart: (() -> Unit)? = null
    var onGlideComplete: ((String) -> Unit)? = null
    var onHaptic: ((isLongPress: Boolean) -> Unit)? = null

    // Configurable state synced from KeyboardPreferences & VietKeyboardService
    var page: Page = Page.LETTERS
        set(value) {
            if (field != value) {
                field = value
                rebuildKeys()
                invalidate()
            }
        }
    var showNumberRow: Boolean = true
    var showLanguageSwitchKey: Boolean = true
    var longPressForSymbols: Boolean = true
    var isDarkTheme: Boolean = false
    var showKeyBorders: Boolean = true
    var isShifted: Boolean = false
    var isCapsLock: Boolean = false
    var isVietnameseMode: Boolean = true
    var spaceLabel: String = "Tiếng Việt"
    var keyHeightScale: Float = 1.0f
    var longPressDelayMs: Long = 300L
    var enableGlideTyping: Boolean = true
    var showGestureTrail: Boolean = true
    var glideThresholdDp: Int = 24
    var enableGestureCursor: Boolean = true
    var enableGestureDelete: Boolean = true
    var popupOnKeypress: Boolean = true
    var langSwitchIconRes: Int = R.drawable.ic_switch_ime_logo

    private val density = resources.displayMetrics.density
    private val scaledDensity = resources.displayMetrics.scaledDensity
    private val handler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())

    private val rows = ArrayList<List<KeySpec>>(5)
    private val allKeys = ArrayList<KeySpec>(44)
    private val pointers = SparseArray<PointerTracker>(4)

    // Glide state
    private var isGliding = false
    private var glidePointerId = -1
    private val glideKeys = StringBuilder()
    private val glideTrailPoints = ArrayList<Pair<Float, Float>>(64)
    private val glidePath = Path()

    // Inline alternates popup (e.g. long-press on '.' or letter with multiple accents)
    private var activeAltOptions: List<String>? = null
    private var activeAltSelectedIdx: Int = 0
    private var activeAltAnchorRect: RectF? = null

    // Backspace repeat state
    private var backspaceRepeating = false
    private var backspaceGestureWords = 0
    private val backspaceRepeatRunnable = object : Runnable {
        override fun run() {
            if (!backspaceRepeating) return
            onBackspaceDown?.invoke()
            handler.postDelayed(this, 50L)
        }
    }

    // Pre-allocated Paints (Zero allocation during onDraw)
    private val keyBgPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
    private val keyShadowPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
    private val primaryTextPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
        typeface = Typeface.create("sans-serif", Typeface.NORMAL)
    }
    private val secondaryTextPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        typeface = Typeface.create("sans-serif", Typeface.NORMAL)
    }
    private val trailPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        color = Color.parseColor("#CC1A73E8")
        style = Paint.Style.STROKE
        strokeWidth = 6f * density
        strokeCap = Paint.Cap.ROUND
        strokeJoin = Paint.Join.ROUND
    }
    private val popupBgPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
    private val popupStrokePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        style = Paint.Style.STROKE
        strokeWidth = 1f * density
        color = Color.parseColor("#331A73E8")
    }
    private val tempRect = RectF()

    private var drawableShift: Drawable? = null
    private var drawableShiftFilled: Drawable? = null
    private var drawableBackspace: Drawable? = null
    private var drawableEnter: Drawable? = null
    private var drawableLangIcon: Drawable? = null
    private var cachedLangIconRes: Int = 0

    init {
        drawableShift = ContextCompat.getDrawable(context, R.drawable.ic_gboard_shift)?.mutate()
        drawableShiftFilled = ContextCompat.getDrawable(context, R.drawable.ic_gboard_shift_filled)?.mutate()
        drawableBackspace = ContextCompat.getDrawable(context, R.drawable.ic_gboard_backspace)?.mutate()
        drawableEnter = ContextCompat.getDrawable(context, R.drawable.ic_gboard_enter)?.mutate()
    }

    fun syncStateAndInvalidate() {
        if (cachedLangIconRes != langSwitchIconRes) {
            cachedLangIconRes = langSwitchIconRes
            drawableLangIcon = ContextCompat.getDrawable(context, langSwitchIconRes)?.mutate()
        }
        rebuildKeys()
        requestLayout()
        invalidate()
    }

    override fun onMeasure(widthMeasureSpec: Int, heightMeasureSpec: Int) {
        val w = MeasureSpec.getSize(widthMeasureSpec)
        val letterRowH = (46f * density * keyHeightScale).toInt()
        val numRowH = (40f * density * keyHeightScale).toInt()
        val hasNumRow = (page == Page.LETTERS && showNumberRow)
        val totalH = (if (hasNumRow) numRowH else 0) + (letterRowH * 4) + (8f * density).toInt()
        setMeasuredDimension(w, totalH)
    }

    override fun onSizeChanged(w: Int, h: Int, oldw: Int, oldh: Int) {
        super.onSizeChanged(w, h, oldw, oldh)
        rebuildKeys()
    }

    private fun rebuildKeys() {
        rows.clear()
        allKeys.clear()
        when (page) {
            Page.LETTERS -> buildLettersPage()
            Page.SYMBOLS1 -> buildSymbols1Page()
            Page.SYMBOLS2 -> buildSymbols2Page()
        }
        layoutKeyRects(width, height)
    }

    private fun buildLettersPage() {
        if (showNumberRow) {
            rows.add(("1234567890").map { d ->
                KeySpec(id = "num_$d", role = KeyRole.NUMBER, primaryLabel = d.toString())
            })
        }
        rows.add(("qwertyuiop").map { ch ->
            val label = if (isShifted || isCapsLock) ch.uppercase() else ch.toString()
            val sec = if (longPressForSymbols) secondaryForLetter(ch) else null
            KeySpec(id = "key_$ch", role = KeyRole.CHAR, primaryLabel = label, secondaryLabel = sec)
        })
        rows.add(("asdfghjkl").map { ch ->
            val label = if (isShifted || isCapsLock) ch.uppercase() else ch.toString()
            val sec = if (longPressForSymbols) secondaryForLetter(ch) else null
            KeySpec(id = "key_$ch", role = KeyRole.CHAR, primaryLabel = label, secondaryLabel = sec)
        })
        val row3 = ArrayList<KeySpec>(9)
        row3.add(KeySpec(id = "key_shift", role = KeyRole.SHIFT, primaryLabel = "⇧", weight = 1.5f))
        for (ch in "zxcvbnm") {
            val label = if (isShifted || isCapsLock) ch.uppercase() else ch.toString()
            val sec = if (longPressForSymbols) secondaryForLetter(ch) else null
            row3.add(KeySpec(id = "key_$ch", role = KeyRole.CHAR, primaryLabel = label, secondaryLabel = sec))
        }
        row3.add(KeySpec(id = "key_backspace", role = KeyRole.BACKSPACE, primaryLabel = "⌫", weight = 1.5f))
        rows.add(row3)

        val row4 = ArrayList<KeySpec>(6)
        row4.add(KeySpec(id = "key_mode_s1", role = KeyRole.MODE_SYMBOLS1, primaryLabel = "?123", weight = 1.5f))
        row4.add(KeySpec(id = "key_comma", role = KeyRole.COMMA, primaryLabel = ",", secondaryLabel = "😊", weight = 0.9f))
        if (showLanguageSwitchKey) {
            row4.add(KeySpec(id = "key_lang", role = KeyRole.SWITCH_LANG, primaryLabel = "🌐", weight = 1.0f))
        }
        row4.add(KeySpec(id = "key_space", role = KeyRole.SPACE, primaryLabel = spaceLabel, weight = 5.2f))
        row4.add(KeySpec(id = "key_dot", role = KeyRole.DOT, primaryLabel = ".", weight = 0.9f))
        row4.add(KeySpec(id = "key_enter", role = KeyRole.ENTER, primaryLabel = "↵", weight = 1.5f))
        rows.add(row4)
    }

    private fun buildSymbols1Page() {
        rows.add(("1234567890").map { KeySpec("s1_$it", KeyRole.NUMBER, it.toString()) })
        rows.add(listOf("@", "#", "$", "_", "&", "-", "+", "(", ")", "/").map { KeySpec("s1_$it", KeyRole.NUMBER, it) })
        val row3 = ArrayList<KeySpec>(9)
        row3.add(KeySpec("key_mode_s2", KeyRole.MODE_SYMBOLS2, "=\\<", weight = 1.5f))
        listOf("*", "\"", "'", ":", ";", "!", "?").forEach { row3.add(KeySpec("s1_$it", KeyRole.NUMBER, it)) }
        row3.add(KeySpec("key_backspace", KeyRole.BACKSPACE, "⌫", weight = 1.5f))
        rows.add(row3)

        val row4 = ArrayList<KeySpec>(6)
        row4.add(KeySpec("key_mode_abc", KeyRole.MODE_LETTERS, "ABC", weight = 1.5f))
        row4.add(KeySpec("s1_comma", KeyRole.COMMA, ",", weight = 0.9f))
        if (showLanguageSwitchKey) row4.add(KeySpec("key_lang", KeyRole.SWITCH_LANG, "🌐", weight = 1.0f))
        row4.add(KeySpec("key_space", KeyRole.SPACE, spaceLabel, weight = 5.2f))
        row4.add(KeySpec("s1_dot", KeyRole.DOT, ".", weight = 0.9f))
        row4.add(KeySpec("key_enter", KeyRole.ENTER, "↵", weight = 1.5f))
        rows.add(row4)
    }

    private fun buildSymbols2Page() {
        rows.add(listOf("~", "`", "|", "•", "√", "π", "÷", "×", "¶", "∆").map { KeySpec("s2_$it", KeyRole.NUMBER, it) })
        rows.add(listOf("£", "¢", "€", "¥", "^", "°", "=", "{", "}", "%").map { KeySpec("s2_$it", KeyRole.NUMBER, it) })
        val row3 = ArrayList<KeySpec>(9)
        row3.add(KeySpec("key_mode_s1", KeyRole.MODE_SYMBOLS1, "?123", weight = 1.5f))
        listOf("\\", "©", "®", "™", "✓", "[", "]").forEach { row3.add(KeySpec("s2_$it", KeyRole.NUMBER, it)) }
        row3.add(KeySpec("key_backspace", KeyRole.BACKSPACE, "⌫", weight = 1.5f))
        rows.add(row3)

        val row4 = ArrayList<KeySpec>(6)
        row4.add(KeySpec("key_mode_abc", KeyRole.MODE_LETTERS, "ABC", weight = 1.5f))
        row4.add(KeySpec("s2_lt", KeyRole.NUMBER, "<", weight = 0.9f))
        if (showLanguageSwitchKey) row4.add(KeySpec("key_lang", KeyRole.SWITCH_LANG, "🌐", weight = 1.0f))
        row4.add(KeySpec("key_space", KeyRole.SPACE, spaceLabel, weight = 5.2f))
        row4.add(KeySpec("s2_gt", KeyRole.NUMBER, ">", weight = 0.9f))
        row4.add(KeySpec("key_enter", KeyRole.ENTER, "↵", weight = 1.5f))
        rows.add(row4)
    }

    private fun layoutKeyRects(totalW: Int, totalH: Int) {
        if (totalW <= 0 || totalH <= 0 || rows.isEmpty()) return
        val letterRowH = 46f * density * keyHeightScale
        val numRowH = 40f * density * keyHeightScale
        val gapX = 2.5f * density
        val gapY = 3.5f * density
        var curY = 2f * density

        rows.forEachIndexed { rIdx, row ->
            val isNumRow = (page == Page.LETTERS && showNumberRow && rIdx == 0)
            val rowH = if (isNumRow) numRowH else letterRowH
            val isIndentedRow2 = (page == Page.LETTERS && ((showNumberRow && rIdx == 2) || (!showNumberRow && rIdx == 1)))
            val sideInset = if (isIndentedRow2) 16f * density else 0f
            val availW = (totalW - sideInset * 2f).coerceAtLeast(1f)
            val totalWeight = row.sumOf { it.weight.toDouble() }.toFloat().coerceAtLeast(1f)

            var curX = sideInset
            for (key in row) {
                val cellW = availW * (key.weight / totalWeight)
                key.hitRect.set(curX, curY, curX + cellW, curY + rowH)
                key.rect.set(curX + gapX, curY + gapY, curX + cellW - gapX, curY + rowH - gapY)
                allKeys.add(key)
                curX += cellW
            }
            curY += rowH
        }
    }

    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
        super.onDraw(canvas)
        val cornerR = 6.5f * density
        val keyColor = context.getColor(if (isDarkTheme) R.color.key_bg_dark else R.color.key_bg)
        val funcColor = context.getColor(if (isDarkTheme) R.color.key_func_bg_dark else R.color.key_func_bg)
        val pressedColor = Color.parseColor(if (isDarkTheme) "#FF4A4E54" else "#FFD0D6DF")
        val enterColor = Color.parseColor("#FF1A73E8")
        val textColor = context.getColor(if (isDarkTheme) R.color.key_text_dark else R.color.key_text)
        val hintColor = context.getColor(R.color.key_hint)
        keyShadowPaint.color = Color.parseColor(if (isDarkTheme) "#55000000" else "#22000000")

        // Collect pressed keys across active pointers
        var pressedKey1: KeySpec? = null
        var pressedKey2: KeySpec? = null
        for (i in 0 until pointers.size()) {
            val k = pointers.valueAt(i).currentKey
            if (pressedKey1 == null) pressedKey1 = k else if (pressedKey2 == null) pressedKey2 = k
        }

        for (key in allKeys) {
            val isPressed = (key === pressedKey1 || key === pressedKey2)
            val r = key.rect
            val isFunc = key.role != KeyRole.CHAR && key.role != KeyRole.NUMBER && key.role != KeyRole.SPACE

            if (showKeyBorders || isFunc || key.role == KeyRole.SPACE || isPressed) {
                if (showKeyBorders && !isPressed) {
                    tempRect.set(r.left, r.top + 1.2f * density, r.right, r.bottom + 1.2f * density)
                    val rad = if (key.role == KeyRole.ENTER || key.role == KeyRole.MODE_SYMBOLS1 || key.role == KeyRole.MODE_LETTERS) r.height() / 2f else cornerR
                    canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, rad, rad, keyShadowPaint)
                }
                keyBgPaint.color = when {
                    isPressed -> pressedColor
                    key.role == KeyRole.ENTER -> enterColor
                    isFunc -> funcColor
                    else -> keyColor
                }
                val rad = if (key.role == KeyRole.ENTER || key.role == KeyRole.MODE_SYMBOLS1 || key.role == KeyRole.MODE_LETTERS) r.height() / 2f else cornerR
                canvas.drawRoundRect(r, rad, rad, keyBgPaint)
            }

            // Draw icon or label
            when (key.role) {
                KeyRole.SHIFT -> {
                    val d = if (isShifted || isCapsLock) drawableShiftFilled else drawableShift
                    drawCenteredDrawable(canvas, d, r, 24f * density, textColor)
                }
                KeyRole.BACKSPACE -> {
                    drawCenteredDrawable(canvas, drawableBackspace, r, 24f * density, textColor)
                }
                KeyRole.ENTER -> {
                    drawCenteredDrawable(canvas, drawableEnter, r, 22f * density, Color.WHITE)
                }
                KeyRole.SWITCH_LANG -> {
                    drawCenteredDrawable(canvas, drawableLangIcon, r, 24f * density, null)
                }
                else -> {
                    primaryTextPaint.color = textColor
                    primaryTextPaint.textSize = when (key.role) {
                        KeyRole.SPACE -> 13.5f * scaledDensity
                        KeyRole.MODE_SYMBOLS1, KeyRole.MODE_SYMBOLS2, KeyRole.MODE_LETTERS -> 13.5f * scaledDensity
                        KeyRole.NUMBER -> 17f * scaledDensity
                        else -> 21f * scaledDensity
                    }
                    val fm = primaryTextPaint.fontMetrics
                    val centerY = r.centerY() - (fm.ascent + fm.descent) / 2f +
                        if (key.role == KeyRole.COMMA && key.secondaryLabel != null) 4f * density else 0f
                    canvas.drawText(key.primaryLabel, r.centerX(), centerY, primaryTextPaint)

                    val sec = key.secondaryLabel
                    if (!sec.isNullOrEmpty()) {
                        secondaryTextPaint.color = hintColor
                        secondaryTextPaint.textSize = 10.5f * scaledDensity
                        val sfm = secondaryTextPaint.fontMetrics
                        if (key.role == KeyRole.COMMA) {
                            secondaryTextPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
                            canvas.drawText(sec, r.centerX(), r.top + 4.5f * density - sfm.ascent, secondaryTextPaint)
                        } else {
                            secondaryTextPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.RIGHT
                            canvas.drawText(sec, r.right - 4f * density, r.top + 3f * density - sfm.ascent, secondaryTextPaint)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Glide trail
        if (isGliding && showGestureTrail && glideTrailPoints.size >= 2) {
            glidePath.reset()
            glidePath.moveTo(glideTrailPoints[0].first, glideTrailPoints[0].second)
            for (i in 1 until glideTrailPoints.size) {
                val prev = glideTrailPoints[i - 1]
                val cur = glideTrailPoints[i]
                glidePath.quadTo(prev.first, prev.second, (prev.first + cur.first) / 2f, (prev.second + cur.second) / 2f)
            }
            canvas.drawPath(glidePath, trailPaint)
        }

        // Key preview popup (floating above pressed letter/number key)
        if (popupOnKeypress && !isGliding && activeAltOptions == null) {
            val pk = pressedKey1
            if (pk != null && (pk.role == KeyRole.CHAR || pk.role == KeyRole.NUMBER || pk.role == KeyRole.DOT)) {
                drawKeyPreviewBubble(canvas, pk, keyColor, textColor)
            }
        }

        // Inline alternates strip (for long-press on '.' or letter accents)
        val alts = activeAltOptions
        val anchor = activeAltAnchorRect
        if (alts != null && anchor != null && alts.isNotEmpty()) {
            drawAlternatesStrip(canvas, alts, activeAltSelectedIdx, anchor, keyColor, textColor)
        }
    }

    private fun drawCenteredDrawable(canvas: Canvas, d: Drawable?, rect: RectF, sizePx: Float, tintColor: Int?) {
        d ?: return
        val half = (sizePx / 2f).toInt()
        val cx = rect.centerX().toInt()
        val cy = rect.centerY().toInt()
        d.setBounds(cx - half, cy - half, cx + half, cy + half)
        if (tintColor != null) d.setTint(tintColor)
        d.draw(canvas)
    }

    private fun drawKeyPreviewBubble(canvas: Canvas, key: KeySpec, bgColor: Int, textColor: Int) {
        val r = key.rect
        val bw = r.width() * 1.25f
        val bh = r.height() * 1.25f
        val left = (r.centerX() - bw / 2f).coerceIn(4f * density, width - bw - 4f * density)
        val top = (r.top - bh - 6f * density).coerceAtLeast(2f * density)
        tempRect.set(left, top, left + bw, top + bh)
        popupBgPaint.color = bgColor
        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, 8f * density, 8f * density, popupBgPaint)
        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, 8f * density, 8f * density, popupStrokePaint)
        primaryTextPaint.color = textColor
        primaryTextPaint.textSize = 26f * scaledDensity
        val fm = primaryTextPaint.fontMetrics
        val cy = tempRect.centerY() - (fm.ascent + fm.descent) / 2f
        canvas.drawText(key.primaryLabel, tempRect.centerX(), cy, primaryTextPaint)
    }

    private fun drawAlternatesStrip(
        canvas: Canvas,
        options: List<String>,
        selectedIdx: Int,
        anchor: RectF,
        bgColor: Int,
        textColor: Int
    ) {
        val cellW = 34f * density
        val cellH = 42f * density
        val stripW = cellW * options.size + 8f * density
        val left = (anchor.centerX() - stripW / 2f).coerceIn(4f * density, (width - stripW - 4f * density).coerceAtLeast(4f * density))
        val top = (anchor.top - cellH - 8f * density).coerceAtLeast(2f * density)
        tempRect.set(left, top, left + stripW, top + cellH)
        popupBgPaint.color = bgColor
        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, 10f * density, 10f * density, popupBgPaint)
        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, 10f * density, 10f * density, popupStrokePaint)

        for (i in options.indices) {
            val cx = left + 4f * density + i * cellW + cellW / 2f
            if (i == selectedIdx) {
                tempRect.set(cx - cellW / 2f + 2f * density, top + 3f * density, cx + cellW / 2f - 2f * density, top + cellH - 3f * density)
                keyBgPaint.color = Color.parseColor("#FF1A73E8")
                canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, 7f * density, 7f * density, keyBgPaint)
            }
            primaryTextPaint.color = if (i == selectedIdx) Color.WHITE else textColor
            primaryTextPaint.textSize = 18f * scaledDensity
            val fm = primaryTextPaint.fontMetrics
            val cy = top + cellH / 2f - (fm.ascent + fm.descent) / 2f
            canvas.drawText(options[i], cx, cy, primaryTextPaint)
        }
    }

    private fun findKeyAt(x: Float, y: Float): KeySpec? {
        for (i in 0 until allKeys.size) {
            val k = allKeys[i]
            if (k.hitRect.contains(x, y)) return k
        }
        return null
    }

    override fun onTouchEvent(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
        val action = event.actionMasked
        val idx = event.actionIndex
        val pid = event.getPointerId(idx)
        val x = event.getX(idx)
        val y = event.getY(idx)

        when (action) {
            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN, MotionEvent.ACTION_POINTER_DOWN -> {
                if (action == MotionEvent.ACTION_POINTER_DOWN && isGliding) {
                    // Second finger tapped while gliding -> cancel glide immediately (2-thumb typing!)
                    isGliding = false
                    glideKeys.clear()
                    glideTrailPoints.clear()
                }
                // Cancel pending long-press on any previous finger (rollover)
                for (i in 0 until pointers.size()) {
                    val prev = pointers.valueAt(i)
                    prev.longPressRunnable?.let { handler.removeCallbacks(it) }
                }
                val key = findKeyAt(x, y)
                val tracker = PointerTracker(
                    pointerId = pid,
                    downKey = key,
                    currentKey = key,
                    startX = x,
                    startY = y,
                    lastX = x,
                    lastY = y
                )
                pointers.put(pid, tracker)
                if (key != null) {
                    handleKeyDown(tracker, key)
                }
                invalidate()
                return true
            }

            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                for (p in 0 until event.pointerCount) {
                    val curPid = event.getPointerId(p)
                    val tracker = pointers.get(curPid) ?: continue
                    val mx = event.getX(p)
                    val my = event.getY(p)
                    handlePointerMove(tracker, mx, my, event.pointerCount)
                    tracker.lastX = mx
                    tracker.lastY = my
                }
                invalidate()
                return true
            }

            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP, MotionEvent.ACTION_POINTER_UP -> {
                val tracker = pointers.get(pid)
                if (tracker != null) {
                    handlePointerUp(tracker)
                    pointers.remove(pid)
                }
                invalidate()
                return true
            }

            MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
                for (i in 0 until pointers.size()) {
                    pointers.valueAt(i).longPressRunnable?.let { handler.removeCallbacks(it) }
                }
                pointers.clear()
                stopBackspaceRepeat()
                isGliding = false
                glideKeys.clear()
                glideTrailPoints.clear()
                activeAltOptions = null
                invalidate()
                return true
            }
        }
        return true
    }

    private fun handleKeyDown(tracker: PointerTracker, key: KeySpec) {
        onHaptic?.invoke(false)
        when (key.role) {
            KeyRole.CHAR -> {
                val ch = key.primaryLabel.firstOrNull() ?: return
                onLetterDown?.invoke(ch)
                tracker.committedOnDown = true
                val sec = key.secondaryLabel
                if (!sec.isNullOrEmpty()) {
                    val r = Runnable {
                        tracker.longPressTriggered = true
                        if (tracker.committedOnDown) {
                            onUndoDownLetter?.invoke()
                            tracker.committedOnDown = false
                        }
                        onHaptic?.invoke(true)
                        onSecondaryCommit?.invoke(sec)
                        invalidate()
                    }
                    tracker.longPressRunnable = r
                    handler.postDelayed(r, longPressDelayMs.coerceAtLeast(220L))
                }
            }
            KeyRole.NUMBER -> {
                onDigitOrSymbol?.invoke(key.primaryLabel)
                tracker.committedOnDown = true
            }
            KeyRole.SPACE -> {
                onSpaceDown?.invoke()
                tracker.committedOnDown = true
                val r = Runnable {
                    if (!tracker.isSpaceSwiping) {
                        tracker.longPressTriggered = true
                        if (tracker.committedOnDown) {
                            onUndoDownSpace?.invoke()
                            tracker.committedOnDown = false
                        }
                        onHaptic?.invoke(true)
                        onSpaceLongPress?.invoke()
                    }
                }
                tracker.longPressRunnable = r
                handler.postDelayed(r, 750L)
            }
            KeyRole.COMMA -> {
                onCommaDown?.invoke()
                tracker.committedOnDown = true
                if (key.secondaryLabel != null) {
                    val r = Runnable {
                        tracker.longPressTriggered = true
                        if (tracker.committedOnDown) {
                            onUndoDownPunctuation?.invoke(',')
                            tracker.committedOnDown = false
                        }
                        onHaptic?.invoke(true)
                        onCommaLongPress?.invoke(key.rect)
                    }
                    tracker.longPressRunnable = r
                    handler.postDelayed(r, longPressDelayMs.coerceAtLeast(250L))
                }
            }
            KeyRole.DOT -> {
                onDotDown?.invoke()
                tracker.committedOnDown = true
                val r = Runnable {
                    tracker.longPressTriggered = true
                    if (tracker.committedOnDown) {
                        onUndoDownPunctuation?.invoke('.')
                        tracker.committedOnDown = false
                    }
                    onHaptic?.invoke(true)
                    activeAltOptions = listOf("?", "!", ",", ":", ";", "-", "(", ")", "/")
                    activeAltSelectedIdx = 0
                    activeAltAnchorRect = key.rect
                    invalidate()
                }
                tracker.longPressRunnable = r
                handler.postDelayed(r, longPressDelayMs.coerceAtLeast(250L))
            }
            KeyRole.BACKSPACE -> {
                backspaceGestureWords = 0
                onBackspaceDown?.invoke()
                backspaceRepeating = true
                handler.removeCallbacks(backspaceRepeatRunnable)
                handler.postDelayed(backspaceRepeatRunnable, 400L)
            }
            KeyRole.SHIFT -> onShiftClick?.invoke()
            KeyRole.SWITCH_LANG -> onSwitchLanguage?.invoke()
            KeyRole.ENTER -> onEnterClick?.invoke()
            KeyRole.MODE_SYMBOLS1 -> page = Page.SYMBOLS1
            KeyRole.MODE_SYMBOLS2 -> page = Page.SYMBOLS2
            KeyRole.MODE_LETTERS -> page = Page.LETTERS
        }
    }

    private fun handlePointerMove(tracker: PointerTracker, mx: Float, my: Float, pointerCount: Int) {
        val downKey = tracker.downKey ?: return

        // 1. Inline alternates strip selection (e.g. long-press on '.')
        val alts = activeAltOptions
        val anchor = activeAltAnchorRect
        if (tracker.longPressTriggered && alts != null && anchor != null) {
            val cellW = 34f * density
            val stripW = cellW * alts.size + 8f * density
            val left = (anchor.centerX() - stripW / 2f).coerceIn(4f * density, (width - stripW - 4f * density).coerceAtLeast(4f * density))
            val idx = ((mx - left - 4f * density) / cellW).toInt().coerceIn(0, alts.lastIndex)
            activeAltSelectedIdx = idx
            return
        }

        // 2. Spacebar gesture cursor control
        if (downKey.role == KeyRole.SPACE && enableGestureCursor) {
            val thresholdPx = 28f * density
            val stepPx = 14f * density
            if (!tracker.isSpaceSwiping && kotlin.math.abs(mx - tracker.startX) > thresholdPx) {
                tracker.isSpaceSwiping = true
                tracker.longPressRunnable?.let { handler.removeCallbacks(it) }
                if (tracker.committedOnDown) {
                    onUndoDownSpace?.invoke()
                    tracker.committedOnDown = false
                }
            }
            if (tracker.isSpaceSwiping) {
                val dx = mx - tracker.lastX
                if (dx > stepPx) {
                    onSpaceCursorMove?.invoke(1)
                } else if (dx < -stepPx) {
                    onSpaceCursorMove?.invoke(-1)
                }
            }
            return
        }

        // 3. Backspace slide-to-select gesture delete
        if (downKey.role == KeyRole.BACKSPACE && enableGestureDelete) {
            val swipeStartPx = 24f * density
            val stepPerWordPx = 36f * density
            val dragLeftPx = tracker.startX - mx
            if (!tracker.isBackspaceSwiping && dragLeftPx > swipeStartPx) {
                tracker.isBackspaceSwiping = true
                stopBackspaceRepeat()
                onHaptic?.invoke(true)
                backspaceGestureWords = 1
                onBackspaceGestureSelect?.invoke(1, true)
            }
            if (tracker.isBackspaceSwiping) {
                val extraPx = (dragLeftPx - swipeStartPx).coerceAtLeast(0f)
                val targetWords = if (dragLeftPx <= swipeStartPx * 0.5f) 0 else 1 + (extraPx / stepPerWordPx).toInt()
                if (targetWords != backspaceGestureWords) {
                    backspaceGestureWords = targetWords
                    onBackspaceGestureSelect?.invoke(targetWords, false)
                }
            }
            return
        }

        // 4. Glide typing across letter keys
        if (page == Page.LETTERS && enableGlideTyping && pointerCount == 1 && downKey.role == KeyRole.CHAR && !tracker.longPressTriggered) {
            val dist = kotlin.math.hypot((mx - tracker.startX).toDouble(), (my - tracker.startY).toDouble()).toFloat()
            if (!isGliding && dist > glideThresholdDp * density) {
                isGliding = true
                glidePointerId = tracker.pointerId
                tracker.longPressRunnable?.let { handler.removeCallbacks(it) }
                if (tracker.committedOnDown) {
                    onGlideStart?.invoke()
                    tracker.committedOnDown = false
                }
                glideKeys.clear()
                glideTrailPoints.clear()
                downKey.primaryLabel.firstOrNull()?.lowercaseChar()?.let { glideKeys.append(it) }
                glideTrailPoints.add(tracker.startX to tracker.startY)
            }
            if (isGliding && glidePointerId == tracker.pointerId) {
                glideTrailPoints.add(mx to my)
                if (glideTrailPoints.size > 64) glideTrailPoints.removeAt(0)
                val overKey = findKeyAt(mx, my)
                if (overKey != null && overKey.role == KeyRole.CHAR) {
                    tracker.currentKey = overKey
                    val ch = overKey.primaryLabel.firstOrNull()?.lowercaseChar()
                    if (ch != null && (glideKeys.isEmpty() || glideKeys.last() != ch)) {
                        glideKeys.append(ch)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun handlePointerUp(tracker: PointerTracker) {
        tracker.longPressRunnable?.let { handler.removeCallbacks(it) }
        val downKey = tracker.downKey

        if (activeAltOptions != null && tracker.longPressTriggered) {
            val chosen = activeAltOptions?.getOrNull(activeAltSelectedIdx)
            activeAltOptions = null
            activeAltAnchorRect = null
            if (!chosen.isNullOrEmpty()) {
                onDigitOrSymbol?.invoke(chosen)
            }
            return
        }

        if (downKey?.role == KeyRole.BACKSPACE) {
            if (tracker.isBackspaceSwiping) {
                onBackspaceGestureFinish?.invoke(backspaceGestureWords, false)
                backspaceGestureWords = 0
            } else {
                val wasRepeating = backspaceRepeating
                stopBackspaceRepeat()
                if (wasRepeating) onBackspaceRepeatEnd?.invoke()
            }
            return
        }

        if (isGliding && glidePointerId == tracker.pointerId) {
            val swiped = glideKeys.toString()
            isGliding = false
            glidePointerId = -1
            glideKeys.clear()
            glideTrailPoints.clear()
            if (swiped.length >= 2) {
                onGlideComplete?.invoke(swiped)
            }
        }
    }

    private fun stopBackspaceRepeat() {
        backspaceRepeating = false
        handler.removeCallbacks(backspaceRepeatRunnable)
    }

    private fun secondaryForLetter(ch: Char): String? = when (ch.lowercaseChar()) {
        'q' -> if (showNumberRow) "%" else "1"
        'w' -> if (showNumberRow) "\\" else "2"
        'e' -> if (showNumberRow) "|" else "3"
        'r' -> if (showNumberRow) "=" else "4"
        't' -> if (showNumberRow) "[" else "5"
        'y' -> if (showNumberRow) "]" else "6"
        'u' -> if (showNumberRow) "<" else "7"
        'i' -> if (showNumberRow) ">" else "8"
        'o' -> if (showNumberRow) "{" else "9"
        'p' -> if (showNumberRow) "}" else "0"
        'a' -> "@"
        's' -> "#"
        'd' -> if (isShifted || isCapsLock) "Đ" else "đ"
        'f' -> "_"
        'g' -> "&"
        'h' -> "-"
        'j' -> "+"
        'k' -> "("
        'l' -> ")"
        'z' -> "*"
        'x' -> "\""
        'c' -> "'"
        'v' -> ":"
        'b' -> ";"
        'n' -> "!"
        'm' -> "?"
        else -> null
    }
}
===== END FILE: app/src/main/java/com/vnchflex/vietkeyboard/ime/KeyboardCanvasView.kt =====

===== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/vnchflex/vietkeyboard/ime/VietKeyboardService.kt =====
package com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.ime

import android.content.ClipboardManager
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.Typeface
import android.inputmethodservice.InputMethodService
import android.media.AudioManager
import android.os.IBinder
import android.text.SpannableString
import android.text.Spanned
import android.text.style.ForegroundColorSpan
import android.text.style.RelativeSizeSpan
import android.view.Gravity
import android.view.HapticFeedbackConstants
import android.view.KeyEvent
import android.view.MotionEvent
import android.view.View
import android.view.inputmethod.EditorInfo
import android.view.inputmethod.InputMethodManager
import android.widget.Button
import android.widget.LinearLayout
import android.widget.PopupWindow
import android.widget.TextView
import com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.R
import com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.engine.VietnameseEngine
import com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.macro.MacroEngine
import com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.macro.MacroStore
import com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.settings.KeyboardPreferences
import com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.settings.SettingsActivity
import com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.suggestion.SuggestionEngine

class VietKeyboardService : InputMethodService() {
    private enum class Mode { LETTERS, SYMBOLS1, SYMBOLS2 }
    private enum class ShiftState { LOWER, SHIFTED, CAPS_LOCK }

    private lateinit var prefs: KeyboardPreferences
    private lateinit var vnEngine: VietnameseEngine
    private lateinit var macroEngine: MacroEngine
    private lateinit var macroStore: MacroStore
    private lateinit var suggestionEngine: SuggestionEngine
    private lateinit var onnxCorrector: com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.engine.OnnxVietnameseCorrector

    private var keyboardView: View? = null
    private var topStripContainer: View? = null
    private var suggestionBar: LinearLayout? = null
    private var canvasView: KeyboardCanvasView? = null
    private var canvasGestureBaseEnd: Int = -1
    private var canvasGestureBeforeText: String = ""

    private var mode = Mode.LETTERS
    private var isVietnameseMode = true
    private val enComposing = StringBuilder()
    private val currentTokenBuffer = StringBuilder()
    private lateinit var clipboardStore: ClipboardStore
    private var clipboardPanelController: ClipboardPanelController? = null
    private var isClipboardPanelOpen = false
    private var shouldReloadDictionaryOnResume = false
    private var clipboardManager: ClipboardManager? = null
    private val clipChangedListener = ClipboardManager.OnPrimaryClipChangedListener {
        if (!prefs.enableClipboardHistory) return@OnPrimaryClipChangedListener
        val clip = clipboardManager?.primaryClip ?: return@OnPrimaryClipChangedListener
        if (clip.itemCount > 0) {
            val text = clip.getItemAt(0).coerceToText(this)?.toString().orEmpty()
            if (text.isNotBlank() && ::clipboardStore.isInitialized) clipboardStore.addClip(text)
        }
    }
    private val learnExecutor: java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService = java.util.concurrent.Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor()
    private val hapticExecutor: java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService = java.util.concurrent.Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor()
    private var shiftState = ShiftState.LOWER
    private var lastShiftClickTime = 0L
    private var lastSpaceClickTime = 0L
    private var isSpacePressed = false
    private var spaceCommittedByRollover = false
    private var lastCommittedChar: Char? = null
    private var prevCommittedChar: Char? = null
    private var justExpandedMacroWithSpace = false
    private var justExpandedMacroBeforeDown = false
    private var justInsertedAutoSpaceAfterPunct = false
    private var lastAutoCorrection: Pair<String, String>? = null
    private var hasTextSelection = false
    private var aiToolsPopup: PopupWindow? = null
    private var pendingRolloverAction: (() -> Unit)? = null
    private var pendingRolloverCancel: (() -> Unit)? = null
    private var pendingSuggestionRunnable: Runnable? = null
    private var lastOwnIcEditMs = 0L
    private var lastBackspaceClickTime = 0L
    private var backspaceRepeating = false
    private var lastGlideCommittedLength = 0
    private var prevCommittedWord2 = ""
    private var prevCommittedWord1 = ""
    private var keyPopup: PopupWindow? = null
    private var keyPopupText: TextView? = null
    private var audioManager: AudioManager? = null
    private var vibrator: android.os.Vibrator? = null
    private val popupHandler = android.os.Handler(android.os.Looper.getMainLooper())
    private var pendingPopupAnchor: View? = null
    private var pendingPopupLabel: String = ""
    private val showPopupRunnable = Runnable {
        val anchor = pendingPopupAnchor ?: return@Runnable
        if (anchor.isAttachedToWindow) showKeyPopup(anchor, pendingPopupLabel)
    }

    private var altPopup: PopupWindow? = null
    private var altPopupRow: LinearLayout? = null
    private val altButtons = mutableListOf<TextView>()
    private var altOptions: List<String> = emptyList()
    private var altSelectedIndex = -1
    private var altAnchor: View? = null

    private val suggestionSlots = mutableListOf<TextView>()
    private val suggestionDividers = mutableListOf<View>()
    private var lastSuggestionKey: String? = null

    private var lastAppliedPrefsKey: String? = null

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        prefs = KeyboardPreferences(this)
        audioManager = getSystemService(Context.AUDIO_SERVICE) as? AudioManager
        vibrator = getSystemService(Context.VIBRATOR_SERVICE) as? android.os.Vibrator
        vnEngine = VietnameseEngine()
        macroStore = MacroStore(this)
        macroEngine = MacroEngine(macroStore.load())
        suggestionEngine = SuggestionEngine(this)
        onnxCorrector = com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.engine.OnnxVietnameseCorrector(this).also { it.initAsync() }
        clipboardStore = ClipboardStore(this)
        syncPreferencesToEngines()
        clipboardManager = (getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE) as? ClipboardManager)?.also { cm ->
            cm.addPrimaryClipChangedListener(clipChangedListener)
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        clipboardManager?.removePrimaryClipChangedListener(clipChangedListener)
        if (::onnxCorrector.isInitialized) onnxCorrector.close()
        hapticExecutor.shutdownNow()
        learnExecutor.shutdown()
        try {
            learnExecutor.awaitTermination(250, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)
        } catch (_: InterruptedException) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt()
        }
        if (::suggestionEngine.isInitialized) suggestionEngine.flushPendingSaves()
        super.onDestroy()
    }

    override fun onCreateInputView(): View {
        val view = layoutInflater.inflate(R.layout.keyboard_view, null)
        keyboardView = view
        topStripContainer = view.findViewById(R.id.top_strip_container)
        suggestionBar = view.findViewById(R.id.suggestion_bar)
        val cv = view.findViewById<KeyboardCanvasView>(R.id.keyboard_canvas)
        canvasView = cv
        setupCanvasKeyboard(cv)
        bindClick(R.id.key_strip_diacritics, ::onStripDiacritics)
        bindClick(R.id.btn_toolbar_clipboard, ::onPasteClipboard)
        bindClick(R.id.btn_toolbar_ai_tools, ::onAiToolsClicked)
        bindClick(R.id.btn_toolbar_voice, ::onVoiceTyping)
        bindClick(R.id.btn_toolbar_settings) { openSettingsSection(null) }
        suggestionSlots.clear()
        suggestionDividers.clear()
        lastSuggestionKey = null
        lastAppliedPrefsKey = null
        initSuggestionSlots()
        applyPreferencesToView()
        showMode(Mode.LETTERS)
        return view
    }

    private fun renderGlideCandidates(candidates: List<String>) {
        if (!prefs.showSuggestionStrip || mode != Mode.LETTERS) return
        if (suggestionSlots.isEmpty()) initSuggestionSlots()
        lastSuggestionKey = "GLIDE:" + candidates.joinToString("\u0002")
        for (i in 0 until 3) {
            val tv = suggestionSlots[i]
            val word = candidates.getOrNull(i)
            if (word == null) {
                tv.visibility = View.INVISIBLE
                tv.text = ""
                tv.setOnClickListener(null)
            } else {
                val cased = if (shiftState == ShiftState.CAPS_LOCK) word.uppercase() else word
                tv.text = cased
                tv.setTextColor(if (i == 0) getColor(R.color.suggestion_primary) else getColor(R.color.key_text))
                tv.setTypeface(null, if (i == 0) Typeface.BOLD else Typeface.NORMAL)
                tv.setOnClickListener {
                    performKeyFeedback(tv)
                    replaceLastGlideWordOrAccept(cased)
                }
                tv.visibility = View.VISIBLE
            }
        }
        suggestionDividers.forEachIndexed { idx, div ->
            val leftVisible = suggestionSlots.getOrNull(idx)?.visibility == View.VISIBLE
            val rightVisible = suggestionSlots.getOrNull(idx + 1)?.visibility == View.VISIBLE
            div.visibility = if (leftVisible && rightVisible) View.VISIBLE else View.INVISIBLE
        }
    }

    private fun replaceLastGlideWordOrAccept(word: String) {
        val ic = currentInputConnection ?: return
        if (lastGlideCommittedLength > 0) {
            ic.beginBatchEdit()
            ic.deleteSurroundingText(lastGlideCommittedLength, 0)
            val newCommitted = "$word "
            ic.commitText(newCommitted, 1)
            ic.endBatchEdit()
            lastGlideCommittedLength = newCommitted.length
            recordCommittedWord(word)
            updateAutoCapitalization()
            updateSuggestions("")
            return
        }
        acceptSuggestion(word)
    }

    override fun onStartInputView(info: EditorInfo?, restarting: Boolean) {
        super.onStartInputView(info, restarting)
        if (shouldReloadDictionaryOnResume) {
            shouldReloadDictionaryOnResume = false
            if (::suggestionEngine.isInitialized) {
                learnExecutor.execute { suggestionEngine.reloadFromStore() }
            }
            if (::onnxCorrector.isInitialized) {
                onnxCorrector.reload()
            }
        }
        syncPreferencesToEngines()
        applyPreferencesToView()
        updateAutoCapitalization()
        updateSuggestions(vnEngine.composingText)
    }

    override fun onStartInput(attribute: EditorInfo?, restarting: Boolean) {
        super.onStartInput(attribute, restarting)
        currentTokenBuffer.clear()
        enComposing.clear()
        lastGlideCommittedLength = 0
        justExpandedMacroWithSpace = false
        lastAutoCorrection = null
        val initStart = attribute?.initialSelStart ?: -1
        val initEnd = attribute?.initialSelEnd ?: -1
        updateSelectionToolbarState(initStart >= 0 && initEnd >= 0 && initStart != initEnd)
        trackCommittedChar(null)
        syncPreferencesToEngines()
        vnEngine.useComposingSpan = !isTelegramPackage(attribute?.packageName ?: currentInputEditorInfo?.packageName)
        currentInputConnection?.let { vnEngine.commit(it) } ?: vnEngine.reset()
        updateSuggestions("")
        showMode(Mode.LETTERS)
    }

    private fun isTelegramPackage(pkg: String?): Boolean {
        val p = pkg?.lowercase() ?: return false
        return p == "org.telegram.messenger" || p == "org.telegram.messenger.web" || p == "org.thunderdog.challegram" || p.startsWith("org.telegram.")
    }

    private fun syncPreferencesToEngines() {
        macroEngine.update(macroStore.load())
        vnEngine.freeTonePlacement = prefs.freeTonePlacement
        vnEngine.modernOrthography = prefs.modernOrthography
        vnEngine.quickTelex = prefs.quickTelex
        vnEngine.relaxedCoda = prefs.relaxedCoda
        vnEngine.southernDialectAndGenZ = prefs.southernDialectAndGenZ
        vnEngine.userWhitelist = prefs.englishWhitelist
    }

    private fun applyPreferencesToView() {
        val root = keyboardView ?: return
        val prefsKey = listOf(
            prefs.showNumberRow, prefs.showSuggestionStrip, prefs.showLanguageSwitchKey,
            prefs.showStripDiacriticsButton, prefs.enableVoiceTyping,
            prefs.themeMode, prefs.keyBorders, prefs.oneHandedMode, prefs.keyboardHeight,
            prefs.longPressForSymbols, prefs.enableGlideTyping, prefs.showGestureTrail,
            prefs.glideSensitivityDp, prefs.langSwitchKeyIcon, isVietnameseMode, shiftState.name
        ).joinToString("|")
        if (prefsKey == lastAppliedPrefsKey) return
        lastAppliedPrefsKey = prefsKey

        topStripContainer?.visibility = if (prefs.showSuggestionStrip) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE
        root.findViewById<View>(R.id.key_strip_diacritics)?.visibility =
            if (prefs.showStripDiacriticsButton) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE
        root.findViewById<View>(R.id.btn_toolbar_voice)?.visibility =
            if (prefs.enableVoiceTyping) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE

        val switchIconRes = when (prefs.langSwitchKeyIcon) {
            "ev" -> if (isVietnameseMode) R.drawable.ic_lang_v else R.drawable.ic_lang_e
            "globe" -> R.drawable.ic_lang_globe
            "vncharkey_logo" -> if (isVietnameseMode) R.drawable.ic_lang_vncharkey_logo else R.drawable.ic_lang_vncharkey_e
            else -> R.drawable.ic_switch_ime_logo
        }
        val spaceLabel = if (isVietnameseMode) getString(R.string.key_space) else "English"
        val isDark = prefs.themeMode == "gboard_dark"
        val showBorders = prefs.keyBorders
        root.setBackgroundColor(getColor(if (isDark) R.color.keyboard_bg_dark else R.color.keyboard_bg))

        val density = resources.displayMetrics.density
        val sidePad = (resources.displayMetrics.widthPixels * 0.16f).toInt()
        val baseHPad = (3 * density).toInt()
        val topPad = (3 * density).toInt()
        val bottomPad = (6 * density).toInt()
        when (prefs.oneHandedMode) {
            "left" -> root.setPadding(baseHPad, topPad, sidePad, bottomPad)
            "right" -> root.setPadding(sidePad, topPad, baseHPad, bottomPad)
            else -> root.setPadding(baseHPad, topPad, baseHPad, bottomPad)
        }
        val scale = prefs.keyHeightScale()

        canvasView?.let { cv ->
            cv.showNumberRow = prefs.showNumberRow
            cv.showLanguageSwitchKey = prefs.showLanguageSwitchKey
            cv.longPressForSymbols = prefs.longPressForSymbols
            cv.isDarkTheme = isDark
            cv.showKeyBorders = showBorders
            cv.isShifted = (shiftState == ShiftState.SHIFTED)
            cv.isCapsLock = (shiftState == ShiftState.CAPS_LOCK)
            cv.isVietnameseMode = isVietnameseMode
            cv.spaceLabel = spaceLabel
            cv.keyHeightScale = scale
            cv.longPressDelayMs = prefs.longPressDelayMs.toLong()
            cv.enableGlideTyping = prefs.enableGlideTyping
            cv.showGestureTrail = prefs.showGestureTrail
            cv.glideThresholdDp = prefs.glideSensitivityDp
            cv.enableGestureCursor = prefs.enableGestureCursorControl
            cv.enableGestureDelete = prefs.enableGestureDelete
            cv.popupOnKeypress = prefs.popupOnKeypress
            cv.langSwitchIconRes = switchIconRes
            cv.syncStateAndInvalidate()
        }
        showMode(mode)
    }

    private fun setupCanvasKeyboard(cv: KeyboardCanvasView) {
        cv.onHaptic = { isLong -> performKeyFeedback(cv, isLongPress = isLong) }
        cv.onLetterDown = { ch ->
            justExpandedMacroBeforeDown = justExpandedMacroWithSpace
            onChar(ch)
            if (shiftState == ShiftState.SHIFTED) {
                shiftState = ShiftState.LOWER
                refreshLetterKeyLabels()
            }
        }
        cv.onUndoDownLetter = { undoDownLetterCommit() }
        cv.onSecondaryCommit = { sec -> onSecondarySymbol(sec) }
        cv.onDigitOrSymbol = { txt ->
            if (txt.length == 1 && txt[0] in ".,;:!?") {
                onPunctuation(txt[0])
            } else {
                onDigitOrSymbol(txt)
            }
        }
        cv.onShiftClick = { onShiftPressed() }
        cv.onBackspaceDown = { onBackspace() }
        cv.onBackspaceRepeatEnd = {
            resyncAfterBackspaceRepeat()
            updateAutoCapitalization()
            updateSuggestions(vnEngine.composingText)
        }
        cv.onBackspaceGestureSelect = { wordsCount, isFirst ->
            val ic = currentInputConnection
            if (ic != null) {
                if (isFirst) {
                    markOwnIcEdit()
                    vnEngine.commit(ic)
                    val extracted = ic.getExtractedText(android.view.inputmethod.ExtractedTextRequest(), 0)
                    if (extracted != null && extracted.selectionEnd >= 0) {
                        canvasGestureBaseEnd = extracted.selectionEnd
                        canvasGestureBeforeText = ic.getTextBeforeCursor(512, 0)?.toString().orEmpty()
                    } else {
                        canvasGestureBaseEnd = -1
                        deleteWordBeforeCursor()
                    }
                }
                if (canvasGestureBaseEnd >= 0) {
                    var idx = canvasGestureBeforeText.length
                    var rem = wordsCount
                    while (rem > 0 && idx > 0) {
                        while (idx > 0 && canvasGestureBeforeText[idx - 1].isWhitespace()) idx--
                        while (idx > 0 && !canvasGestureBeforeText[idx - 1].isWhitespace()) idx--
                        rem--
                    }
                    val delLen = canvasGestureBeforeText.length - idx
                    val selStart = (canvasGestureBaseEnd - delLen).coerceAtLeast(0)
                    markOwnIcEdit()
                    ic.setSelection(selStart, canvasGestureBaseEnd)
                }
            }
        }
        cv.onBackspaceGestureFinish = { wordsCount, cancelled ->
            val ic = currentInputConnection
            if (ic != null && canvasGestureBaseEnd >= 0) {
                if (!cancelled && wordsCount > 0) {
                    markOwnIcEdit()
                    ic.commitText("", 1)
                    currentTokenBuffer.clear()
                    justExpandedMacroWithSpace = false
                    updateAutoCapitalization()
                    updateSuggestions("")
                } else {
                    markOwnIcEdit()
                    ic.setSelection(canvasGestureBaseEnd, canvasGestureBaseEnd)
                }
            }
            canvasGestureBaseEnd = -1
        }
        cv.onSpaceDown = { onSpacePressed() }
        cv.onUndoDownSpace = { undoDownSpaceCommit() }
        cv.onSpaceCursorMove = { delta -> moveCursor(delta) }
        cv.onSpaceLongPress = { showImePicker() }
        cv.onCommaDown = { onPunctuation(',') }
        cv.onCommaLongPress = { _ -> showEmojiPickerPopup(cv) }
        cv.onDotDown = { onPunctuation('.') }
        cv.onUndoDownPunctuation = { ch -> undoDownPunctuationCommit(ch) }
        cv.onSwitchLanguage = { switchKeyboard() }
        cv.onEnterClick = { onEnter() }
        cv.onGlideStart = {
            dismissKeyPopup()
            dismissAltPopup()
            currentInputConnection?.let { ic ->
                if (vnEngine.isComposing) {
                    vnEngine.onBackspace(ic)
                } else if (!isVietnameseMode && enComposing.isNotEmpty()) {
                    enComposing.deleteCharAt(enComposing.length - 1)
                    ic.deleteSurroundingText(1, 0)
                }
                if (currentTokenBuffer.isNotEmpty()) {
                    currentTokenBuffer.deleteCharAt(currentTokenBuffer.length - 1)
                }
            }
        }
        cv.onGlideComplete = { swipedKeys ->
            dismissKeyPopup()
            val ic = currentInputConnection
            if (ic != null && mode == Mode.LETTERS) {
                vnEngine.commit(ic)
                val candidates = GlideDecoder.decode(
                    swipedKeys = swipedKeys,
                    suggestionEngine = suggestionEngine,
                    prevWord1 = prevCommittedWord1,
                    prevWord2 = prevCommittedWord2,
                    limit = 3,
                    isVietnameseMode = isVietnameseMode
                )
                val best = candidates.firstOrNull()
                if (best != null) {
                    val out = if (shiftState != ShiftState.LOWER) {
                        best.replaceFirstChar { it.uppercaseChar() }
                    } else best
                    val committedText = "$out "
                    ic.commitText(committedText, 1)
                    lastGlideCommittedLength = committedText.length
                    recordCommittedWord(out)
                    if (shiftState == ShiftState.SHIFTED) {
                        shiftState = ShiftState.LOWER
                        refreshLetterKeyLabels()
                    }
                    updateAutoCapitalization()
                    renderGlideCandidates(candidates)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun refreshLetterKeyLabels() {
        canvasView?.let { cv ->
            cv.isShifted = (shiftState == ShiftState.SHIFTED)
            cv.isCapsLock = (shiftState == ShiftState.CAPS_LOCK)
            cv.syncStateAndInvalidate()
        }
    }

    private fun onShiftPressed() {
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        shiftState = when (shiftState) {
            ShiftState.LOWER -> ShiftState.SHIFTED
            ShiftState.SHIFTED -> if (now - lastShiftClickTime < 400L) ShiftState.CAPS_LOCK else ShiftState.LOWER
            ShiftState.CAPS_LOCK -> ShiftState.LOWER
        }
        lastShiftClickTime = now
        refreshLetterKeyLabels()
    }

    private fun updateAutoCapitalization(beforeText: String? = null) {
        if (!prefs.autoCapitalization || shiftState == ShiftState.CAPS_LOCK) return
        val shouldCap = if (beforeText != null) {
            val trimmed = beforeText.trimEnd()
            beforeText.isEmpty() || trimmed.endsWith(".") || trimmed.endsWith("!") || trimmed.endsWith("?") || trimmed.endsWith("\n")
        } else {
            lastCommittedChar == null || lastCommittedChar == '\n' ||
                (lastCommittedChar == ' ' && (prevCommittedChar == '.' || prevCommittedChar == '!' || prevCommittedChar == '?'))
        }
        val newShift = if (shouldCap) ShiftState.SHIFTED else ShiftState.LOWER
        if (shiftState != newShift) {
            shiftState = newShift
            refreshLetterKeyLabels()
        }
    }

    private fun bindClick(id: Int, action: () -> Unit) {
        keyboardView?.findViewById<View>(id)?.setOnClickListener { view ->
            performKeyFeedback(view)
            action()
        }
    }

    private fun bindRepeat(id: Int, action: () -> Unit) {
        val view = keyboardView?.findViewById<View>(id) ?: return
        var startX = 0f
        var didSwipeDelete = false
        var repeating = false
        var gestureBaseEnd = -1
        var gestureBeforeText = ""
        var currentSelectedWords = 0
        val repeatHandler = android.os.Handler(android.os.Looper.getMainLooper())
        lateinit var repeatRunnable: Runnable
        repeatRunnable = object : Runnable {
            override fun run() {
                if (didSwipeDelete) return
                repeating = true
                backspaceRepeating = true
                action()
                repeatHandler.postDelayed(this, 50L)
            }
        }

        fun wordDeleteLength(before: String, wordCount: Int): Int {
            if (wordCount <= 0 || before.isEmpty()) return 0
            var idx = before.length
            var remaining = wordCount
            while (remaining > 0 && idx > 0) {
                while (idx > 0 && before[idx - 1].isWhitespace()) idx--
                while (idx > 0 && !before[idx - 1].isWhitespace()) idx--
                remaining--
            }
            return before.length - idx
        }

        view.setOnClickListener(null)
        view.setOnLongClickListener(null)
        view.setOnTouchListener { _, event ->
            val density = resources.displayMetrics.density
            val swipeStartPx = 24f * density
            val stepPerWordPx = 36f * density
            when (event.actionMasked) {
                MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                    startX = event.x
                    didSwipeDelete = false
                    repeating = false
                    backspaceRepeating = false
                    gestureBaseEnd = -1
                    gestureBeforeText = ""
                    currentSelectedWords = 0
                    performKeyFeedback(view)
                    action()
                    repeatHandler.removeCallbacks(repeatRunnable)
                    repeatHandler.postDelayed(repeatRunnable, 400L)
                    true
                }
                MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                    if (!prefs.enableGestureDelete) return@setOnTouchListener true
                    val dragLeftPx = startX - event.x
                    if (!didSwipeDelete && dragLeftPx > swipeStartPx) {
                        didSwipeDelete = true
                        backspaceRepeating = false
                        repeatHandler.removeCallbacks(repeatRunnable)
                        performKeyFeedback(view, isLongPress = true)
                        val ic = currentInputConnection
                        if (ic != null) {
                            markOwnIcEdit()
                            vnEngine.commit(ic)
                            val extracted = ic.getExtractedText(android.view.inputmethod.ExtractedTextRequest(), 0)
                            if (extracted != null && extracted.selectionEnd >= 0) {
                                gestureBaseEnd = extracted.selectionEnd
                                gestureBeforeText = ic.getTextBeforeCursor(512, 0)?.toString().orEmpty()
                            } else {
                                deleteWordBeforeCursor()
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if (didSwipeDelete && gestureBaseEnd >= 0) {
                        val extraPx = (dragLeftPx - swipeStartPx).coerceAtLeast(0f)
                        val targetWords = if (dragLeftPx <= swipeStartPx * 0.5f) {
                            0
                        } else {
                            1 + (extraPx / stepPerWordPx).toInt()
                        }
                        if (targetWords != currentSelectedWords) {
                            currentSelectedWords = targetWords
                            val delLen = wordDeleteLength(gestureBeforeText, targetWords)
                            val selStart = (gestureBaseEnd - delLen).coerceAtLeast(0)
                            markOwnIcEdit()
                            currentInputConnection?.setSelection(selStart, gestureBaseEnd)
                        }
                    }
                    true
                }
                MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
                    repeatHandler.removeCallbacks(repeatRunnable)
                    if (didSwipeDelete && gestureBaseEnd >= 0) {
                        val ic = currentInputConnection
                        if (ic != null && currentSelectedWords > 0) {
                            markOwnIcEdit()
                            ic.commitText("", 1)
                            currentTokenBuffer.clear()
                            justExpandedMacroWithSpace = false
                            updateAutoCapitalization()
                            updateSuggestions("")
                        } else if (ic != null) {
                            markOwnIcEdit()
                            ic.setSelection(gestureBaseEnd, gestureBaseEnd)
                        }
                        didSwipeDelete = false
                        gestureBaseEnd = -1
                        return@setOnTouchListener true
                    }
                    if (backspaceRepeating) {
                        backspaceRepeating = false
                        resyncAfterBackspaceRepeat()
                        updateAutoCapitalization()
                        updateSuggestions(vnEngine.composingText)
                    }
                    true
                }
                MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
                    repeatHandler.removeCallbacks(repeatRunnable)
                    if (didSwipeDelete && gestureBaseEnd >= 0) {
                        markOwnIcEdit()
                        currentInputConnection?.setSelection(gestureBaseEnd, gestureBaseEnd)
                    }
                    didSwipeDelete = false
                    gestureBaseEnd = -1
                    backspaceRepeating = false
                    true
                }
            }
            true
        }
    }

    private fun showMode(newMode: Mode) {
        mode = newMode
        canvasView?.visibility = View.VISIBLE
        canvasView?.page = when (newMode) {
            Mode.LETTERS -> KeyboardCanvasView.Page.LETTERS
            Mode.SYMBOLS1 -> KeyboardCanvasView.Page.SYMBOLS1
            Mode.SYMBOLS2 -> KeyboardCanvasView.Page.SYMBOLS2
        }
    }

    private fun onDigitOrSymbol(text: String) {
        commitRaw(text)
    }

    private fun onSecondarySymbol(symbol: String) {
        val ic = currentInputConnection ?: return
        // Nếu ký hiệu phụ là chữ cái tiếng Việt (vd: phím D có ký hiệu phụ 'đ'/'Đ' chuẩn Gboard Tiếng Việt),
        // tự động khớp chữ HOA/thường theo trạng thái Shift và đưa vào bộ gõ.
        if (symbol.length == 1 && symbol[0].isLetter()) {
            val ch = if (shiftState != ShiftState.LOWER) symbol.uppercase()[0] else symbol.lowercase()[0]
            onChar(ch)
            if (shiftState == ShiftState.SHIFTED) {
                shiftState = ShiftState.LOWER
                refreshLetterKeyLabels()
            }
            return
        }
        commitRaw(symbol)
    }

    private fun flushPendingRollover() {
        val action = pendingRolloverAction
        val cancel = pendingRolloverCancel
        pendingRolloverAction = null
        pendingRolloverCancel = null
        cancel?.invoke()
        action?.invoke()
    }

    private fun trackCommittedChar(c: Char?) {
        if (c == null) {
            prevCommittedChar = null
            lastCommittedChar = null
        } else {
            prevCommittedChar = lastCommittedChar
            lastCommittedChar = c
        }
    }

    private fun scheduleSuggestionsUpdate(word: String) {
        pendingSuggestionRunnable?.let { popupHandler.removeCallbacks(it) }
        val runnable = Runnable { updateSuggestions(word) }
        pendingSuggestionRunnable = runnable
        popupHandler.postDelayed(runnable, 25L)
    }

    private fun onSpacePressed(): Boolean {
        val ic = currentInputConnection ?: return false
        markOwnIcEdit()
        if (justInsertedAutoSpaceAfterPunct) {
            justInsertedAutoSpaceAfterPunct = false
            return false
        }
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        // Gboard Double-space period option (không cần IPC getTextBeforeCursor)
        if (prefs.doubleSpacePeriod && now - lastSpaceClickTime < 350L && !vnEngine.isComposing) {
            if (lastCommittedChar == ' ' && prevCommittedChar?.isLetterOrDigit() == true) {
                ic.beginBatchEdit()
                ic.deleteSurroundingText(1, 0)
                ic.commitText(". ", 1)
                ic.endBatchEdit()
                trackCommittedChar('.')
                trackCommittedChar(' ')
                lastSpaceClickTime = 0L
                updateAutoCapitalization()
                scheduleSuggestionsUpdate("")
                return true
            }
        }
        lastSpaceClickTime = now
        onChar(' ')
        return true
    }
