    private fun onPunctuation(c: Char) {
        val ic = currentInputConnection
        ic?.beginBatchEdit()
        if (justExpandedMacroWithSpace && c in ".,;:!?") {
            justExpandedMacroWithSpace = false
            ic?.deleteSurroundingText(1, 0)
        }
        onChar(c)
        if (prefs.autoSpaceAfterPunctuation && (c == ',' || c == '.' || c == '!' || c == '?')) {
            ic?.commitText(" ", 1)
            trackCommittedChar(' ')
            justInsertedAutoSpaceAfterPunct = true
        } else {
            justInsertedAutoSpaceAfterPunct = false
        }
        ic?.endBatchEdit()
        lastSpaceClickTime = 0L
        updateAutoCapitalization()
    }

    private fun onChar(c: Char) {
        if (c != ' ') {
            justInsertedAutoSpaceAfterPunct = false
        }
        // Space rollover: nếu ngón tay vẫn đang giữ Space (chưa swipe/long-press) mà chạm phím khác, chốt Space ngay
        if (c != ' ' && isSpacePressed && !spaceCommittedByRollover) {
            isSpacePressed = false
            val committed = onSpacePressed()
            spaceCommittedByRollover = committed
        }
        lastGlideCommittedLength = 0
        if (altPopup?.isShowing == true) dismissAltPopup()
        val ic = currentInputConnection ?: return
        markOwnIcEdit()
        if (mode != Mode.LETTERS) {
            commitRaw(c.toString())
            return
        }
        if (!isVietnameseMode) {
            if (isMacroTrigger(c)) {
                val finishedEn = enComposing.toString()
                val tokenForMacro = if (currentTokenBuffer.isNotEmpty()) currentTokenBuffer.toString() else finishedEn
                enComposing.clear()
                currentTokenBuffer.clear()
                if (finishedEn.isNotEmpty()) {
                    recordCommittedWord(finishedEn)
                }
                if (prefs.macroEnabled && tokenForMacro.isNotEmpty() && macroEngine.tryExpand(ic, c, beforeCursor = tokenForMacro)) {
                    justExpandedMacroWithSpace = (c == ' ')
                    trackCommittedChar(c)
                    updateAutoCapitalization()
                    updateSuggestions("")
                    return
                }
                justExpandedMacroWithSpace = false
                ic.commitText(c.toString(), 1)
                trackCommittedChar(c)
                updateAutoCapitalization()
                updateSuggestions("")
                return
            }
            if (c.isLetterOrDigit() || c == '\'') {
                if (c.isLetter() || c == '\'') enComposing.append(c) else enComposing.clear()
                currentTokenBuffer.append(c)
            } else {
                enComposing.clear()
                currentTokenBuffer.clear()
            }
            ic.commitText(c.toString(), 1)
            trackCommittedChar(c)
            updateAutoCapitalization()
            updateSuggestions(enComposing.toString())
            return
        }
        if (isMacroTrigger(c)) {
            val finishedWord = vnEngine.composingText
            val tokenForMacro = if (currentTokenBuffer.isNotEmpty()) currentTokenBuffer.toString() else finishedWord
            val composingLen = finishedWord.length
            currentTokenBuffer.clear()
            if (prefs.macroEnabled && tokenForMacro.isNotEmpty() &&
                macroEngine.tryExpand(ic, c, beforeCursor = tokenForMacro, composingLength = composingLen)
            ) {
                if (finishedWord.isNotEmpty()) recordCommittedWord(finishedWord)
                vnEngine.reset() // composing span cleared by tryExpand
                justExpandedMacroWithSpace = (c == ' ')
                lastAutoCorrection = null
                trackCommittedChar(c)
                updateAutoCapitalization()
                scheduleSuggestionsUpdate("")
                return
            }
            if (c == ' ' && prefs.autoCorrection && finishedWord.length in 2..7) {
                val corrected = suggestionEngine.findSpatialAutoCorrection(
                    typedWord = finishedWord,
                    prevWord1 = prevCommittedWord1,
                    prevWord2 = prevCommittedWord2,
                    whitelist = prefs.englishWhitelist
                )
                if (corrected != null && corrected != finishedWord) {
                    val replacement = "$corrected "
                    ic.beginBatchEdit()
                    vnEngine.reset(ic)
                    ic.commitText(replacement, 1)
                    ic.endBatchEdit()
                    lastAutoCorrection = finishedWord to replacement
                    justExpandedMacroWithSpace = false
                    recordCommittedWord(corrected)
                    trackCommittedChar(' ')
                    updateAutoCapitalization()
                    scheduleSuggestionsUpdate("")
                    return
                }
            }
            if (finishedWord.isNotEmpty()) {
                recordCommittedWord(finishedWord)
            }
            lastAutoCorrection = null
            vnEngine.commit(ic)
            justExpandedMacroWithSpace = false
            if (vnEngine.useComposingSpan) {
                ic.beginBatchEdit()
                ic.commitText(c.toString(), 1)
                ic.endBatchEdit()
            } else {
                ic.commitText(c.toString(), 1)
            }
            trackCommittedChar(c)
            updateAutoCapitalization()
            scheduleSuggestionsUpdate("")
            return
        }

        lastAutoCorrection = null
        justExpandedMacroWithSpace = false
        val handled = vnEngine.onChar(c, ic)
        if (!handled) {
            vnEngine.commit(ic)
            ic.commitText(c.toString(), 1)
        }
        if (c.isLetterOrDigit()) {
            currentTokenBuffer.append(c)
        } else {
            currentTokenBuffer.clear()
        }
        trackCommittedChar(c)
        scheduleSuggestionsUpdate(vnEngine.composingText)
    }

    private fun commitRaw(text: String) {
        lastGlideCommittedLength = 0
        justExpandedMacroWithSpace = false
        if (altPopup?.isShowing == true) dismissAltPopup()
        val ic = currentInputConnection ?: return
        markOwnIcEdit()
        vnEngine.commit(ic)
        ic.commitText(text, 1)
        if (text.isNotEmpty() && text.all { it.isLetterOrDigit() }) {
            currentTokenBuffer.append(text)
        } else {
            currentTokenBuffer.clear()
        }
        trackCommittedChar(text.lastOrNull())
        updateAutoCapitalization()
        updateSuggestions("")
    }

    private fun resyncAfterBackspaceRepeat() {
        val ic = currentInputConnection ?: return
        if (mode != Mode.LETTERS || !isVietnameseMode) {
            vnEngine.reset()
            return
        }
        if (vnEngine.isComposing) return
        val before = ic.getTextBeforeCursor(32, 0)?.toString().orEmpty()
        val prevWord = before.takeLastWhile { it.isLetter() }
        if (prevWord.isNotEmpty()) {
            vnEngine.rehydrateWord(prevWord, ic)
        } else {
            vnEngine.reset()
        }
    }

    private fun onBackspace() {
        val ic = currentInputConnection ?: return
        markOwnIcEdit()
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        justExpandedMacroWithSpace = false
        if (lastGlideCommittedLength > 0 && !backspaceRepeating) {
            currentTokenBuffer.clear()
        } else if (currentTokenBuffer.isNotEmpty()) {
            currentTokenBuffer.deleteCharAt(currentTokenBuffer.length - 1)
        }

        // 0. Gboard Glide Typing behavior: Single Backspace immediately after a glided word deletes the whole glided word
        if (lastGlideCommittedLength > 0 && !backspaceRepeating) {
            ic.deleteSurroundingText(lastGlideCommittedLength, 0)
            lastGlideCommittedLength = 0
            syncContextFromEditor()
            updateAutoCapitalization()
            updateSuggestions("")
            return
        }
        lastGlideCommittedLength = 0

        // 0b. Undo spatial auto-correction on immediate Backspace (Gboard behavior)
        val autoCorr = lastAutoCorrection
        if (autoCorr != null && prefs.undoAutoCorrectOnBackspace && !backspaceRepeating) {
            val (origWord, committedReplacement) = autoCorr
            lastAutoCorrection = null
            ic.beginBatchEdit()
            ic.deleteSurroundingText(committedReplacement.length, 0)
            ic.commitText("$origWord ", 1)
            ic.endBatchEdit()
            recordCommittedWord(origWord, forceImmediate = true)
            trackCommittedChar(' ')
            updateAutoCapitalization()
            scheduleSuggestionsUpdate("")
            return
        }
        lastAutoCorrection = null

        // 1. Double-backspace to undo last macro expansion (e.g. "Hà Nội " -> "hn ")
        val lastMacro = macroEngine.lastExpansion
        if (lastMacro != null) {
            if (now - lastBackspaceClickTime < 600L && !backspaceRepeating) {
                val (orig, expanded) = lastMacro
                val before = ic.getTextBeforeCursor(expanded.length + 2, 0)?.toString().orEmpty()
                // After first backspace, 1 char was already deleted from `expanded`
                val expandedAfterOneDelete = expanded.dropLast(1)
                if (expandedAfterOneDelete.isNotEmpty() && before.endsWith(expandedAfterOneDelete)) {
                    ic.beginBatchEdit()
                    ic.deleteSurroundingText(expandedAfterOneDelete.length, 0)
                    ic.commitText(orig, 1)
                    ic.endBatchEdit()
                    macroEngine.clearLastExpansion()
                    lastBackspaceClickTime = 0L
                    scheduleSuggestionsUpdate("")
                    return
                }
            } else if (now - lastBackspaceClickTime >= 600L) {
                macroEngine.clearLastExpansion()
            }
        }
        lastBackspaceClickTime = now

        // 2. Normal Vietnamese composing backspace
        if (mode == Mode.LETTERS && isVietnameseMode && vnEngine.onBackspace(ic)) {
            if (!backspaceRepeating) scheduleSuggestionsUpdate(vnEngine.composingText)
            return
        }
        if (mode == Mode.LETTERS && !isVietnameseMode && enComposing.isNotEmpty()) {
            enComposing.deleteCharAt(enComposing.length - 1)
            ic.deleteSurroundingText(1, 0)
            if (!backspaceRepeating) {
                updateSuggestions(enComposing.toString())
                updateAutoCapitalization()
            }
            return
        }

        // 3. Delete 1 char; if we land on a word, rehydrate it as composing in one batch
        if (!backspaceRepeating && mode == Mode.LETTERS && isVietnameseMode) {
            val before = ic.getTextBeforeCursor(33, 0)?.toString().orEmpty()
            if (before.isNotEmpty()) {
                val afterDel = before.dropLast(1)
                val prevWord = afterDel.takeLastWhile { it.isLetter() }
                if (prevWord.isNotEmpty()) {
                    ic.deleteSurroundingText(1, 0)
                    vnEngine.rehydrateWord(prevWord, ic)
                    scheduleSuggestionsUpdate(vnEngine.composingText)
                    updateAutoCapitalization(afterDel)
                    return
                }
            }
        }
        ic.deleteSurroundingText(1, 0)
        if (!backspaceRepeating) {
            updateAutoCapitalization()
            scheduleSuggestionsUpdate("")
        }
    }

    private fun moveCursor(delta: Int) {
        val ic = currentInputConnection ?: return
        val keyCode = if (delta < 0) KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DPAD_LEFT else KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DPAD_RIGHT
        ic.sendKeyEvent(KeyEvent(KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN, keyCode))
        ic.sendKeyEvent(KeyEvent(KeyEvent.ACTION_UP, keyCode))
    }

    private fun alternatesForLetter(base: String): List<String> {
        val b = base.lowercase()
        val sec = secondarySymbolForLetter(b)
        val list = mutableListOf<String>()
        if (sec != null) list.add(sec)
        val extras = when (b) {
            "a" -> listOf("á", "à", "ả", "ã", "ạ", "ă", "â")
            "e" -> listOf("é", "è", "ẻ", "ẽ", "ẹ", "ê")
            "i" -> listOf("í", "ì", "ỉ", "ĩ", "ị")
            "o" -> listOf("ó", "ò", "ỏ", "õ", "ọ", "ô", "ơ")
            "u" -> listOf("ú", "ù", "ủ", "ũ", "ụ", "ư")
            "y" -> listOf("ý", "ỳ", "ỷ", "ỹ", "ỵ")
            "d" -> listOf("đ")
            "c" -> listOf("ç")
            "n" -> listOf("ñ")
            "s" -> listOf("ß")
            else -> emptyList()
        }
        for (e in extras) {
            val cased = if (shiftState != ShiftState.LOWER) e.uppercase() else e
            if (cased != sec && cased.lowercase() != b) list.add(cased)
        }
        return list.distinct()
    }

    private fun showAlternatesPopup(anchor: View, options: List<String>) {
        dismissKeyPopup()
        altOptions = options
        altSelectedIndex = 0
        altAnchor = anchor

        val row = altPopupRow ?: LinearLayout(this).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL
            setBackgroundResource(R.drawable.popup_bg)
            elevation = 10f
            val p = (4 * resources.displayMetrics.density).toInt()
            setPadding(p, p, p, p)
            altPopupRow = this
        }
        row.removeAllViews()
        altButtons.clear()

        val padH = (12 * resources.displayMetrics.density).toInt()
        val padV = (8 * resources.displayMetrics.density).toInt()
        options.forEachIndexed { idx, opt ->
            val tv = TextView(this).apply {
                text = opt
                textSize = 20f
                gravity = Gravity.CENTER
                minWidth = (36 * resources.displayMetrics.density).toInt()
                setPadding(padH, padV, padH, padV)
                setTextColor(getColor(R.color.key_text))
            }
            altButtons.add(tv)
            row.addView(tv)
        }
        updateAltHighlight()

        val popup = altPopup ?: PopupWindow(
            row,
            LinearLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            LinearLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            false
        ).also { altPopup = it }
        if (popup.contentView !== row) popup.contentView = row
        popup.isClippingEnabled = false

        row.measure(View.MeasureSpec.UNSPECIFIED, View.MeasureSpec.UNSPECIFIED)
        row.layout(0, 0, row.measuredWidth, row.measuredHeight)
        val loc = IntArray(2)
        anchor.getLocationInWindow(loc)
        val popupW = row.measuredWidth
        val popupH = row.measuredHeight
        val screenW = resources.displayMetrics.widthPixels
        val x = (loc[0] + anchor.width / 2 - popupW / 2).coerceIn(0, (screenW - popupW).coerceAtLeast(0))
        val y = (loc[1] - popupH - 8).coerceAtLeast(0)
        if (popup.isShowing) popup.dismiss()
        popup.showAtLocation(anchor, Gravity.NO_GRAVITY, x, y)
    }

    private fun updateAltHighlight() {
        altButtons.forEachIndexed { i, tv ->
            if (i == altSelectedIndex) {
                tv.setBackgroundColor(getColor(R.color.accent))
                tv.setTextColor(getColor(R.color.enter_text))
            } else {
                tv.setBackgroundColor(Color.TRANSPARENT)
                tv.setTextColor(getColor(R.color.key_text))
            }
        }
    }

    private fun updateAltSelectionFromTouch(anchor: View, event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
        if (altPopup?.isShowing != true || altAnchor !== anchor || altButtons.isEmpty()) return false
        val row = altPopupRow ?: return false
        val rowLoc = IntArray(2)
        row.getLocationOnScreen(rowLoc)
        val touchXOnScreen = event.rawX.toInt()
        val relativeX = touchXOnScreen - rowLoc[0]
        var idx = altButtons.indexOfFirst { btn ->
            val left = btn.left
            val right = btn.right
            relativeX in left..right
        }
        if (idx < 0) {
            idx = when {
                relativeX < 0 -> 0
                relativeX > row.width -> altButtons.lastIndex
                else -> altSelectedIndex
            }
        }
        if (idx != altSelectedIndex) {
            altSelectedIndex = idx
            updateAltHighlight()
        }
        return true
    }

    private fun commitAltSelection(): Boolean {
        val popup = altPopup ?: return false
        if (!popup.isShowing) return false
        val sel = altOptions.getOrNull(altSelectedIndex) ?: return false
        popup.dismiss()
        altOptions = emptyList()
        altButtons.clear()
        altSelectedIndex = -1
        altAnchor = null
        commitPopupSelection(sel)
        return true
    }

    private fun dismissAltPopup() {
        altPopup?.dismiss()
        altOptions = emptyList()
        altButtons.clear()
        altSelectedIndex = -1
        altAnchor = null
    }

    private fun showEmojiPickerPopup(anchor: View) {
        dismissKeyPopup()
        dismissAltPopup()
        val density = resources.displayMetrics.density
        val emojis = listOf(
            "😊", "😂", "🤣", "❤️", "😍", "🥰", "😘", "😭",
            "😢", "😅", "😎", "🤔", "😴", "😡", "🥵", "🥶",
            "👍", "👎", "👌", "🙏", "👏", "🤝", "✌️", "💪",
            "🔥", "✨", "🎉", "🎂", "🎁", "⭐", "💯", "🎶",
            "🍺", "🍻", "☕", "🍵", "🍚", "🍜", "🍰", "🍷",
            "☔", "☀️", "🌤️", "🌧️", "🌸", "🐶", "🐱", "🏠",
            "🚗", "✈️", "📱", "💻", "📷", "📚", "💰", "⏰"
        )
        val grid = android.widget.GridLayout(this).apply {
            columnCount = 8
            setPadding((8 * density).toInt(), (8 * density).toInt(), (8 * density).toInt(), (8 * density).toInt())
        }
        var popupRef: PopupWindow? = null
        val cellSize = (40 * density).toInt()
        for (emoji in emojis) {
            val tv = TextView(this).apply {
                text = emoji
                textSize = 22f
                gravity = android.view.Gravity.CENTER
                layoutParams = android.widget.GridLayout.LayoutParams().apply {
                    width = cellSize
                    height = cellSize
                }
                setOnClickListener {
                    performKeyFeedback(this)
                    commitRaw(emoji)
                    popupRef?.dismiss()
                }
            }
            grid.addView(tv)
        }
        val scroll = android.widget.ScrollView(this).apply {
            addView(grid)
            setBackgroundColor(getColor(R.color.keyboard_bg))
            elevation = 8 * density
        }
        val widthPx = (336 * density).toInt()
        val heightPx = (210 * density).toInt()
        val popup = PopupWindow(scroll, widthPx, heightPx, false).apply {
            isOutsideTouchable = true
            isTouchable = true
        }
        popupRef = popup
        popup.showAtLocation(anchor.rootView, android.view.Gravity.BOTTOM or android.view.Gravity.CENTER_HORIZONTAL, 0, (56 * density).toInt())
    }

    private fun undoDownLetterCommit() {
        val ic = currentInputConnection ?: return
        markOwnIcEdit()
        if (mode == Mode.LETTERS && isVietnameseMode) {
            if (!vnEngine.onBackspace(ic)) {
                ic.deleteSurroundingText(1, 0)
            }
        } else {
            ic.deleteSurroundingText(1, 0)
            if (enComposing.isNotEmpty()) enComposing.deleteCharAt(enComposing.length - 1)
        }
        if (currentTokenBuffer.isNotEmpty()) currentTokenBuffer.deleteCharAt(currentTokenBuffer.length - 1)
        lastCommittedChar = prevCommittedChar
        prevCommittedChar = null
        justExpandedMacroWithSpace = justExpandedMacroBeforeDown
    }

    private fun undoDownSpaceCommit() {
        val ic = currentInputConnection ?: return
        markOwnIcEdit()
        ic.deleteSurroundingText(1, 0)
        lastCommittedChar = prevCommittedChar
        prevCommittedChar = null
    }

    private fun undoDownPunctuationCommit(punct: Char) {
        val ic = currentInputConnection ?: return
        markOwnIcEdit()
        val deleteLen = if (prefs.autoSpaceAfterPunctuation && punct in ",.!?") 2 else 1
        ic.deleteSurroundingText(deleteLen, 0)
        lastCommittedChar = prevCommittedChar
        prevCommittedChar = null
    }

    private fun commitPopupSelection(text: String) {
        if (text.length == 1) {
            val c = text[0]
            if (c.isLetter()) {
                val ch = if (shiftState != ShiftState.LOWER) c.uppercaseChar() else c.lowercaseChar()
                onChar(ch)
                if (shiftState == ShiftState.SHIFTED) {
                    shiftState = ShiftState.LOWER
                    refreshLetterKeyLabels()
                }
                return
            }
            if (c in ".,;:!?") {
                onPunctuation(c)
                return
            }
        }
        commitRaw(text)
    }

    private fun scheduleKeyPopup(anchor: View, label: String) {
        popupHandler.removeCallbacks(showPopupRunnable)
        pendingPopupAnchor = anchor
        pendingPopupLabel = label
        val delay = if (prefs.enableGlideTyping) 65L else 35L
        popupHandler.postDelayed(showPopupRunnable, delay)
    }

    private fun showKeyPopup(anchor: View, label: String) {
        val tv = keyPopupText ?: TextView(this).apply {
            textSize = 26f
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER
            setTextColor(getColor(R.color.key_text))
            setBackgroundResource(R.drawable.key_bg)
            setPadding(28, 14, 28, 14)
            elevation = 8f
            keyPopupText = this
        }
        tv.text = label
        val popup = keyPopup ?: PopupWindow(
            tv,
            LinearLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            LinearLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            false
        ).also { keyPopup = it }
        if (popup.contentView !== tv) popup.contentView = tv
        val loc = IntArray(2)
        anchor.getLocationInWindow(loc)
        val y = (loc[1] - anchor.height - 16).coerceAtLeast(0)
        if (popup.isShowing) popup.dismiss()
        popup.showAtLocation(anchor, Gravity.NO_GRAVITY, loc[0], y)
    }

    private fun dismissKeyPopup() {
        popupHandler.removeCallbacks(showPopupRunnable)
        pendingPopupAnchor = null
        keyPopup?.let { if (it.isShowing) it.dismiss() }
    }

    private fun deleteWordBeforeCursor() {
        currentTokenBuffer.clear()
        justExpandedMacroWithSpace = false
        val ic = currentInputConnection ?: return
        markOwnIcEdit()
        if (vnEngine.isComposing) {
            vnEngine.reset(ic)
            updateSuggestions("")
            return
        }
        val before = ic.getTextBeforeCursor(64, 0)?.toString().orEmpty()
        if (before.isEmpty()) return
        val trailingSpaces = before.takeLastWhile { it.isWhitespace() }.length
        val wordLen = before.dropLast(trailingSpaces).takeLastWhile { !it.isWhitespace() }.length
        val totalDelete = (trailingSpaces + wordLen).coerceAtLeast(1)
        ic.deleteSurroundingText(totalDelete, 0)
        updateAutoCapitalization()
        updateSuggestions("")
    }

    private fun onEnter() {
        currentTokenBuffer.clear()
        justExpandedMacroWithSpace = false
        lastGlideCommittedLength = 0
        trackCommittedChar('\n')
        val ic = currentInputConnection ?: return
        markOwnIcEdit()
        vnEngine.commit(ic)
        ic.sendKeyEvent(KeyEvent(KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN, KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER))
        ic.sendKeyEvent(KeyEvent(KeyEvent.ACTION_UP, KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER))
        updateAutoCapitalization()
        updateSuggestions("")
    }

    private fun onStripDiacritics() {
        val ic = currentInputConnection ?: return
        val selected = ic.getSelectedText(0)?.toString().orEmpty()
        if (selected.isNotEmpty()) {
            markOwnIcEdit()
            ic.commitText(com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.engine.VietnameseTextNormalizer.stripDiacritics(selected), 1)
            updateSelectionToolbarState(false)
            return
        }
        vnEngine.removeDiacritics(ic)
        updateSuggestions(vnEngine.composingText)
    }

    private fun updateSelectionToolbarState(selected: Boolean) {
        hasTextSelection = selected
        keyboardView?.findViewById<View>(R.id.btn_toolbar_ai_tools)?.visibility =
            if (selected) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE
        if (!selected) {
            dismissAiToolsPopup()
        }
    }

    private fun dismissAiToolsPopup() {
        aiToolsPopup?.let { if (it.isShowing) it.dismiss() }
        aiToolsPopup = null
    }

    private fun onAiToolsClicked() {
        if (aiToolsPopup?.isShowing == true) {
            dismissAiToolsPopup()
            return
        }
        val ic = currentInputConnection ?: return
        val selected = ic.getSelectedText(0)?.toString().orEmpty()
        if (selected.isEmpty()) {
            updateSelectionToolbarState(false)
            return
        }
        val anchor = keyboardView?.findViewById<View>(R.id.btn_toolbar_ai_tools)
            ?: keyboardView
            ?: return
        val density = resources.displayMetrics.density

        val container = LinearLayout(this).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.VERTICAL
            background = android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable().apply {
                shape = android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable.RECTANGLE
                cornerRadius = 14f * density
                setColor(getColor(R.color.keyboard_bg))
                setStroke((1 * density).toInt(), Color.parseColor("#331A73E8"))
            }
            elevation = 10f * density
            setPadding((10 * density).toInt(), (8 * density).toInt(), (10 * density).toInt(), (8 * density).toInt())
        }

        val actions: List<Pair<String, (String) -> String>> = listOf(
            "✨  Khôi phục dấu Tiếng Việt" to { txt ->
                if (::onnxCorrector.isInitialized && onnxCorrector.isReady) {
                    onnxCorrector.correctSentence(txt)
                } else {
                    com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.engine.VietnameseTextNormalizer.restoreDiacriticsAndNormalize(txt, suggestionEngine)
                }
            },
            "📝  Chuẩn hóa dấu câu & viết hoa" to { txt ->
                com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.engine.VietnameseTextNormalizer.normalizePunctuationAndCase(txt)
            },
            "🔤  Bỏ dấu Tiếng Việt" to { txt ->
                com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.engine.VietnameseTextNormalizer.stripDiacritics(txt)
            },
            "🔠  Đổi IN HOA / chữ thường" to { txt ->
                com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.engine.VietnameseTextNormalizer.cycleCase(txt)
            }
        )

        for ((label, transformer) in actions) {
            val btn = TextView(this).apply {
                text = label
                textSize = 13.5f
                setTypeface(null, Typeface.BOLD)
                setTextColor(getColor(R.color.key_text))
                background = android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable().apply {
                    shape = android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable.RECTANGLE
                    cornerRadius = 10f * density
                    setColor(getColor(R.color.key_bg))
                }
                setPadding((12 * density).toInt(), (10 * density).toInt(), (12 * density).toInt(), (10 * density).toInt())
                layoutParams = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
                    LinearLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
                    LinearLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT
                ).apply {
                    topMargin = (3 * density).toInt()
                    bottomMargin = (3 * density).toInt()
                }
                setOnClickListener {
                    performKeyFeedback(this)
                    val currentSel = currentInputConnection?.getSelectedText(0)?.toString().orEmpty()
                    val target = if (currentSel.isNotEmpty()) currentSel else selected
                    val transformed = transformer(target)
                    if (transformed.isNotEmpty()) {
                        markOwnIcEdit()
                        currentInputConnection?.commitText(transformed, 1)
                    }
                    dismissAiToolsPopup()
                    updateSelectionToolbarState(false)
                }
            }
            container.addView(btn)
        }

        val popup = PopupWindow(
            container,
            (265 * density).toInt(),
            LinearLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            false
        ).apply {
            isOutsideTouchable = true
            isTouchable = true
        }
        aiToolsPopup = popup
        popup.showAtLocation(
            anchor.rootView,
            Gravity.BOTTOM or Gravity.CENTER_HORIZONTAL,
            0,
            (54 * density).toInt()
        )
    }

    private fun onVoiceTyping() {
        currentInputConnection?.let { vnEngine.commit(it) }
        // 1. Try switching to an enabled system Voice Input IME (e.g. Google Voice Typing) if available
        val imm = getSystemService(INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
        val voiceImeId = imm?.enabledInputMethodList?.firstOrNull { imi ->
            imi.packageName != packageName && (
                imi.id.contains("voice", ignoreCase = true) ||
                    imi.packageName.contains("tts", ignoreCase = true) ||
                    (0 until imi.subtypeCount).any { idx ->
                        imi.getSubtypeAt(idx).mode.equals("voice", ignoreCase = true)
                    }
                )
        }?.id
        if (voiceImeId != null) {
            try {
                switchInputMethod(voiceImeId)
                return
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                // Fallback to VoiceInputActivity below
            }
        }

        // 2. Fallback: launch translucent VoiceInputActivity using RecognizerIntent.ACTION_RECOGNIZE_SPEECH
        VoiceInputActivity.onVoiceResult = { spokenText ->
            popupHandler.post {
                val trimmed = spokenText.trim()
                if (trimmed.isNotEmpty()) {
                    commitRaw("$trimmed ")
                    recordCommittedWord(trimmed)
                }
            }
        }
        val intent = Intent(this, VoiceInputActivity::class.java).apply {
            addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP)
            putExtra(VoiceInputActivity.EXTRA_LANG, if (isVietnameseMode) "vi-VN" else "en-US")
        }
        startActivity(intent)
    }

    private fun captureCurrentSystemClip() {
        if (!prefs.enableClipboardHistory) return
        val cm = clipboardManager ?: (getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE) as? ClipboardManager)?.also { clipboardManager = it }
        val current = cm?.primaryClip?.takeIf { it.itemCount > 0 }?.getItemAt(0)?.coerceToText(this)?.toString().orEmpty()
        if (current.isNotBlank()) {
            clipboardStore.addClip(current)
        }
    }

    private fun onPasteClipboard() {
        val root = keyboardView?.findViewById<LinearLayout>(R.id.keyboard_root) ?: return
        if (isClipboardPanelOpen) {
            closeClipboardPanel()
            return
        }
        captureCurrentSystemClip()

        val controller = clipboardPanelController ?: ClipboardPanelController(
            context = this,
            prefs = prefs,
            store = clipboardStore,
            onPaste = { text -> commitRaw(text) },
            onClose = { closeClipboardPanel() },
            onCaptureCurrentClip = { captureCurrentSystemClip() },
            onHaptic = { view -> performKeyFeedback(view) }
        ).also { clipboardPanelController = it }

        val targetHeight = root.height
        root.findViewById<View>(R.id.top_strip_container)?.visibility = View.GONE
        canvasView?.visibility = View.GONE

        val panel = controller.createOrUpdateView(targetHeight)
        if (panel.parent !== root) {
            (panel.parent as? android.view.ViewGroup)?.removeView(panel)
            root.addView(panel)
        }
        panel.visibility = View.VISIBLE
        isClipboardPanelOpen = true
    }

    private fun closeClipboardPanel() {
        val root = keyboardView?.findViewById<LinearLayout>(R.id.keyboard_root) ?: return
        clipboardPanelController?.resetEditMode()
        root.removeView(clipboardPanelController?.createOrUpdateView(0))
        root.findViewById<View>(R.id.top_strip_container)?.visibility =
            if (prefs.showSuggestionStrip) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE
        showMode(mode)
        isClipboardPanelOpen = false
    }

    private fun openSettingsSection(section: String?) {
        shouldReloadDictionaryOnResume = true
        val intent = Intent(this, SettingsActivity::class.java).apply {
            addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
            if (section != null) putExtra("section", section)
        }
        startActivity(intent)
    }

    /**
     * Gboard behavior: Tap language switch key toggles Tiếng Việt <-> English.
     * Long-press Spacebar opens the system IME Picker.
     */
    private fun switchKeyboard() {
        currentInputConnection?.let { vnEngine.commit(it) }
        isVietnameseMode = !isVietnameseMode
        if (mode != Mode.LETTERS) showMode(Mode.LETTERS)
        applyPreferencesToView()
        updateSuggestions("")
    }

    private fun showImePicker() {
        val imm = getSystemService(INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as InputMethodManager
        imm.showInputMethodPicker()
    }

    private fun performKeyFeedback(view: View, isLongPress: Boolean = false) {
        if (prefs.hapticOnKeypress) {
            val ms = prefs.vibrationStrengthMs
            if (ms > 0) {
                if (ms != 15 && android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= android.os.Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                    val vib = vibrator ?: (getSystemService(Context.VIBRATOR_SERVICE) as? android.os.Vibrator)?.also { vibrator = it }
                    val effect = android.os.VibrationEffect.createOneShot(
                        ms.toLong(),
                        android.os.VibrationEffect.DEFAULT_AMPLITUDE
                    )
                    hapticExecutor.execute { vib?.vibrate(effect) }
                } else {
                    val flag = if (isLongPress) HapticFeedbackConstants.LONG_PRESS else HapticFeedbackConstants.KEYBOARD_TAP
                    view.performHapticFeedback(flag)
                }
            }
        }
        if (prefs.soundOnKeypress) {
            val am = audioManager ?: (getSystemService(Context.AUDIO_SERVICE) as? AudioManager)?.also { audioManager = it }
            val vol = (prefs.soundVolume / 100f).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
            am?.playSoundEffect(AudioManager.FX_KEY_CLICK, vol)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Bảng ký hiệu phụ chuẩn Gboard Tiếng Việt dành riêng cho 26 phím chữ cái:
     * - Khi đã bật Hàng phím số (showNumberRow = true), hàng Q..P hiển thị bộ ký hiệu Gboard: % \ | = [ ] < > { }
     * - Khi tắt Hàng phím số (showNumberRow = false), hàng Q..P hiển thị số: 1 2 3 4 5 6 7 8 9 0
     * - Hàng A..L: @ # đ _ & - + ( )
     * - Hàng Z..M: * " ' : ; ! ?
     */
    private fun secondarySymbolForLetter(letterKey: String): String? = when (letterKey.lowercase()) {
        // Hàng chữ trên (QWERTYUIOP)
        "q" -> if (prefs.showNumberRow) "%" else "1"
        "w" -> if (prefs.showNumberRow) "\\" else "2"
        "e" -> if (prefs.showNumberRow) "|" else "3"
        "r" -> if (prefs.showNumberRow) "=" else "4"
        "t" -> if (prefs.showNumberRow) "[" else "5"
        "y" -> if (prefs.showNumberRow) "]" else "6"
        "u" -> if (prefs.showNumberRow) "<" else "7"
        "i" -> if (prefs.showNumberRow) ">" else "8"
        "o" -> if (prefs.showNumberRow) "{" else "9"
        "p" -> if (prefs.showNumberRow) "}" else "0"
        // Hàng chữ giữa (ASDFGHJKL) - chuẩn Gboard Tiếng Việt (D -> đ, F -> _)
        "a" -> "@"
        "s" -> "#"
        "d" -> if (shiftState != ShiftState.LOWER) "Đ" else "đ"
        "f" -> "_"
        "g" -> "&"
        "h" -> "-"
        "j" -> "+"
        "k" -> "("
        "l" -> ")"
        // Hàng chữ dưới (ZXCVBNM)
        "z" -> "*"
        "x" -> "\""
        "c" -> "'"
        "v" -> ":"
        "b" -> ";"
        "n" -> "!"
        "m" -> "?"
        else -> null
    }

    private fun initSuggestionSlots() {
        val bar = suggestionBar ?: return
        if (suggestionSlots.isNotEmpty()) return
        bar.removeAllViews()
        for (i in 0 until 3) {
            if (i > 0) {
                val divider = View(this).apply {
                    layoutParams = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(2, 42)
                    setBackgroundColor(getColor(R.color.strip_divider))
                    visibility = View.INVISIBLE
                }
                suggestionDividers.add(divider)
                bar.addView(divider)
            }
            val tv = TextView(this).apply {
                gravity = Gravity.CENTER
                maxLines = 1
                layoutParams = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, LinearLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, 1f)
                textSize = 15f
                visibility = View.INVISIBLE
            }
            suggestionSlots.add(tv)
            bar.addView(tv)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Gboard 3-slot suggestion strip with Trigram Next-Word & Context-Aware Word Suggestions.
     * Reuses fixed slots to avoid view churn on every keystroke.
     */
    private fun updateSuggestions(prefix: String) {
        val bar = suggestionBar ?: return
        if (suggestionSlots.isEmpty()) initSuggestionSlots()
        if (!prefs.showSuggestionStrip || mode != Mode.LETTERS) {
            if (lastSuggestionKey != null) {
                suggestionSlots.forEach { it.visibility = View.INVISIBLE; it.text = "" }
                suggestionDividers.forEach { it.visibility = View.INVISIBLE }
                lastSuggestionKey = null
            }
            return
        }

        val macroPreview = if (prefs.macroEnabled && prefix.isNotEmpty()) {
            macroEngine.lookupPreview(prefix)
        } else null

        val suggestions = when {
            prefix.isNotEmpty() -> suggestionEngine.suggest(
                prefix = prefix,
                prevWord1 = prevCommittedWord1,
                prevWord2 = prevCommittedWord2,
                limit = 3,
                includeEmoji = prefs.showEmojiSuggestions,
                macroPreview = macroPreview,
            )
            prefs.nextWordSuggestions -> suggestionEngine.suggestNextWord(
                prevWord1 = prevCommittedWord1,
                prevWord2 = prevCommittedWord2,
                limit = 3,
            )
            else -> emptyList()
        }

        val key = prefix + "\u0001" + prevCommittedWord2 + "\u0001" + prevCommittedWord1 + "\u0001" + suggestions.joinToString("\u0002")
        if (key == lastSuggestionKey) return
        lastSuggestionKey = key

        for (i in 0 until 3) {
            val tv = suggestionSlots[i]
            val word = suggestions.getOrNull(i)
            if (word == null) {
                tv.visibility = View.INVISIBLE
                tv.text = ""
                tv.setOnClickListener(null)
            } else {
                val isPrimaryCenter = (i == 1 && prefix.isNotEmpty()) || (macroPreview != null && word == macroPreview)
                tv.text = word
                tv.setTextColor(
                    if (isPrimaryCenter) getColor(R.color.suggestion_primary)
                    else getColor(R.color.key_text)
                )
                tv.setTypeface(null, if (isPrimaryCenter) Typeface.BOLD else Typeface.NORMAL)
                tv.setOnClickListener {
                    performKeyFeedback(tv)
                    acceptSuggestion(word)
                }
                tv.visibility = View.VISIBLE
            }
        }
        suggestionDividers.forEachIndexed { idx, div ->
            val leftVisible = suggestionSlots.getOrNull(idx)?.visibility == View.VISIBLE
            val rightVisible = suggestionSlots.getOrNull(idx + 1)?.visibility == View.VISIBLE
            div.visibility = if (leftVisible && rightVisible) View.VISIBLE else View.INVISIBLE
        }
    }

    private fun acceptSuggestion(word: String) {
        currentTokenBuffer.clear()
        justExpandedMacroWithSpace = false
        val ic = currentInputConnection ?: return
        markOwnIcEdit()
        val composing = vnEngine.composingText
        if (composing.isNotEmpty()) {
            // Replace composing span with suggestion
            vnEngine.reset(ic)
            ic.commitText("$word ", 1)
        } else if (!isVietnameseMode && enComposing.isNotEmpty()) {
            val len = enComposing.length
            enComposing.clear()
            ic.beginBatchEdit()
            ic.deleteSurroundingText(len, 0)
            ic.commitText("$word ", 1)
            ic.endBatchEdit()
        } else {
            vnEngine.reset(ic)
            val before = ic.getTextBeforeCursor(32, 0)?.toString().orEmpty()
            val prefix = before.takeLastWhile { it.isLetter() }
            ic.beginBatchEdit()
            if (prefix.isNotEmpty()) ic.deleteSurroundingText(prefix.length, 0)
            ic.commitText("$word ", 1)
            ic.endBatchEdit()
        }
        recordCommittedWord(word, forceImmediate = true)
        trackCommittedChar(' ')
        updateAutoCapitalization()
        updateSuggestions("")
    }

    private fun isSensitiveInputField(): Boolean {
        val info = currentInputEditorInfo ?: return false
        if ((info.imeOptions and EditorInfo.IME_FLAG_NO_PERSONALIZED_LEARNING) != 0) return true
        val inputClass = info.inputType and android.text.InputType.TYPE_MASK_CLASS
        val variation = info.inputType and android.text.InputType.TYPE_MASK_VARIATION
        if (inputClass == android.text.InputType.TYPE_CLASS_NUMBER &&
            variation == android.text.InputType.TYPE_NUMBER_VARIATION_PASSWORD
        ) return true
        if (inputClass == android.text.InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT) {
            if (variation == android.text.InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_PASSWORD ||
                variation == android.text.InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_VISIBLE_PASSWORD ||
                variation == android.text.InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_WEB_PASSWORD ||
                variation == android.text.InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_EMAIL_ADDRESS ||
                variation == android.text.InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_WEB_EMAIL_ADDRESS ||
                variation == android.text.InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_URI
            ) return true
        }
        return false
    }

    private fun recordCommittedWord(word: String, forceImmediate: Boolean = false) {
        if (isSensitiveInputField()) return
        val clean = word.trim()
        if (clean.isEmpty()) return
        val tokens = clean.split(Regex("\\s+")).filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
        if (!::suggestionEngine.isInitialized) return
        for (token in tokens) {
            val p1 = prevCommittedWord1
            val p2 = prevCommittedWord2
            learnExecutor.execute {
                suggestionEngine.learn(token, prevWord1 = p1, prevWord2 = p2, forceImmediate = forceImmediate)
            }
            prevCommittedWord2 = prevCommittedWord1
            prevCommittedWord1 = token
        }
    }

    private fun syncContextFromEditor() {
        val ic = currentInputConnection ?: return
        val before = ic.getTextBeforeCursor(64, 0)?.toString().orEmpty()
        val committedPart = if (vnEngine.isComposing && before.endsWith(vnEngine.composingText)) {
            before.dropLast(vnEngine.composingText.length)
        } else {
            before
        }
        val tokens = committedPart
            .trim()
            .split(Regex("[\\s.,;:!?()\\[\\]\"']+"))
            .filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
        prevCommittedWord1 = tokens.getOrNull(tokens.size - 1).orEmpty()
        prevCommittedWord2 = tokens.getOrNull(tokens.size - 2).orEmpty()
    }

    private fun isMacroTrigger(c: Char): Boolean = c.isWhitespace() || c in ".,;:!?\n"

    private fun markOwnIcEdit() { lastOwnIcEditMs = System.currentTimeMillis() }

    override fun onUpdateSelection(
        oldSelStart: Int, oldSelEnd: Int,
        newSelStart: Int, newSelEnd: Int,
        candidatesStart: Int, candidatesEnd: Int
    ) {
        super.onUpdateSelection(oldSelStart, oldSelEnd, newSelStart, newSelEnd, candidatesStart, candidatesEnd)
        val isSelected = newSelStart >= 0 && newSelEnd >= 0 && newSelStart != newSelEnd
        if (isSelected != hasTextSelection) {
            updateSelectionToolbarState(isSelected)
        }
        if (oldSelStart == newSelStart && oldSelEnd == newSelEnd) return
        if (System.currentTimeMillis() - lastOwnIcEditMs < 200L) return
        val ic = currentInputConnection
        if (newSelStart != newSelEnd) {
            if (vnEngine.isComposing) {
                if (ic != null) vnEngine.commit(ic) else vnEngine.reset()
            } else {
                vnEngine.reset()
            }
            enComposing.clear()
            currentTokenBuffer.clear()
            updateSuggestions("")
            return
        }
        if (vnEngine.isComposing) {
            if (ic != null) vnEngine.commit(ic) else vnEngine.reset()
        }
        enComposing.clear()
        currentTokenBuffer.clear()
        syncContextFromEditor()
        updateSuggestions("")
    }
}
===== END FILE: app/src/main/java/com/vnchflex/vietkeyboard/ime/VietKeyboardService.kt =====

===== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/vnchflex/vietkeyboard/ime/VnchSpellCheckerService.kt =====
package com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.ime

import android.service.textservice.SpellCheckerService
import android.view.textservice.SuggestionsInfo
import android.view.textservice.TextInfo
import com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.suggestion.SuggestionEngine

/**
 * Bilingual Vietnamese + English SpellCheckerService for vnchFlex.
 * Prevents Android's default spell checker from flagging valid Vietnamese words
 * or English loanwords/contractions as typos.
 */
class VnchSpellCheckerService : SpellCheckerService() {

    private lateinit var suggestionEngine: SuggestionEngine

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        suggestionEngine = SuggestionEngine(this)
    }

    override fun createSession(): Session = VnchSession(suggestionEngine)

    private class VnchSession(
        private val engine: SuggestionEngine
    ) : Session() {

        override fun onCreate() {}

        override fun onGetSuggestions(textInfo: TextInfo?, suggestionsLimit: Int): SuggestionsInfo {
            val word = textInfo?.text?.trim().orEmpty()
            if (word.length < 2 || engine.isKnownWord(word)) {
                return SuggestionsInfo(
                    SuggestionsInfo.RESULT_ATTR_IN_THE_DICTIONARY,
                    emptyArray()
                )
            }
            val suggestions = engine.suggest(
                prefix = word,
                prevWord1 = "",
                prevWord2 = "",
                limit = suggestionsLimit.coerceIn(1, 5),
                includeEmoji = false
            ).filter { !it.equals(word, ignoreCase = true) }.toTypedArray()

            val attr = if (suggestions.isNotEmpty()) {
                SuggestionsInfo.RESULT_ATTR_LOOKS_LIKE_TYPO or SuggestionsInfo.RESULT_ATTR_HAS_RECOMMENDED_SUGGESTIONS
            } else {
                SuggestionsInfo.RESULT_ATTR_LOOKS_LIKE_TYPO
            }
            return SuggestionsInfo(attr, suggestions)
        }
    }
}
===== END FILE: app/src/main/java/com/vnchflex/vietkeyboard/ime/VnchSpellCheckerService.kt =====

===== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/vnchflex/vietkeyboard/ime/VoiceInputActivity.kt =====
package com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.ime

import android.app.Activity
import android.content.ActivityNotFoundException
import android.content.Intent
import android.os.Bundle
import android.speech.RecognizerIntent
import android.widget.Toast

/**
 * Lightweight translucent host activity that launches Android's system Speech-to-Text
 * recognizer dialog and passes the recognized text back to [VietKeyboardService].
 */
class VoiceInputActivity : Activity() {

    companion object {
        const val EXTRA_LANG = "extra_lang"
        @Volatile
        var onVoiceResult: ((String) -> Unit)? = null
        private const val REQ_CODE_SPEECH = 9001
    }

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        val lang = intent?.getStringExtra(EXTRA_LANG) ?: "vi-VN"
        val speechIntent = Intent(RecognizerIntent.ACTION_RECOGNIZE_SPEECH).apply {
            putExtra(RecognizerIntent.EXTRA_LANGUAGE_MODEL, RecognizerIntent.LANGUAGE_MODEL_FREE_FORM)
            putExtra(RecognizerIntent.EXTRA_LANGUAGE, lang)
            putExtra(RecognizerIntent.EXTRA_LANGUAGE_PREFERENCE, lang)
            putExtra(RecognizerIntent.EXTRA_PARTIAL_RESULTS, false)
            putExtra(
                RecognizerIntent.EXTRA_PROMPT,
                if (lang.startsWith("vi")) "Đang nghe (Tiếng Việt)..." else "Listening (English)..."
            )
        }
        try {
            startActivityForResult(speechIntent, REQ_CODE_SPEECH)
        } catch (_: ActivityNotFoundException) {
            Toast.makeText(
                this,
                "Thiết bị chưa cài đặt dịch vụ nhận diện giọng nói (Google Voice)",
                Toast.LENGTH_SHORT
            ).show()
            finish()
        }
    }

    @Deprecated("Deprecated in Java")
    override fun onActivityResult(requestCode: Int, resultCode: Int, data: Intent?) {
        super.onActivityResult(requestCode, resultCode, data)
        if (requestCode == REQ_CODE_SPEECH && resultCode == RESULT_OK && data != null) {
            val matches = data.getStringArrayListExtra(RecognizerIntent.EXTRA_RESULTS)
            val best = matches?.firstOrNull { it.isNotBlank() }
            if (!best.isNullOrBlank()) {
                onVoiceResult?.invoke(best)
            }
        }
        finish()
    }
}

package com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.macro

/** A text-expansion rule triggered by a shortcut token. */
data class Macro(
    val shortcut: String,
    val expansion: String,
    val caseSensitive: Boolean = false,
    val enabled: Boolean = true,
)
===== END FILE: app/src/main/java/com/vnchflex/vietkeyboard/macro/Macro.kt =====

===== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/vnchflex/vietkeyboard/macro/MacroEngine.kt =====
package com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.macro

import android.view.inputmethod.InputConnection

/**
 * Macro replacement engine with vnchKey smart case adaptation
 * (e.g. "hn" -> "Hà Nội", "HN" -> "HÀ NỘI", "dc" -> "được", "Dc" -> "Được", "DC" -> "ĐƯỢC")
 * and technical delimiter suppression (e.g. `.vn`, `user@hn`, `file_dc.txt`).
 */
class MacroEngine(initialMacros: List<Macro> = emptyList()) {
    @Volatile
    private var macros: List<Macro> = initialMacros

    /** Stores (originalTokenWithTrigger, expandedTextWithTrigger) for double-backspace undo */
    var lastExpansion: Pair<String, String>? = null
        private set

    fun clearLastExpansion() {
        lastExpansion = null
    }

    fun update(newMacros: List<Macro>) {
        macros = newMacros
    }

    fun tryExpand(
        ic: InputConnection,
        trigger: Char,
        beforeCursor: String? = null,
        composingLength: Int = 0
    ): Boolean {
        if (isTechnicalDelimiter(trigger) || trigger in "([{<") return false

        val before = beforeCursor ?: ic.getTextBeforeCursor(MAX_LOOKBACK, 0)?.toString() ?: return false
        val token = before.takeLastWhile { !it.isWhitespace() && !isTechnicalDelimiter(it) }
        if (token.isEmpty()) return false

        val charBeforeTokenIdx = before.length - token.length - 1
        if (charBeforeTokenIdx >= 0 && isTechnicalDelimiter(before[charBeforeTokenIdx])) {
            return false
        }

        val macro = findMacro(token) ?: return false
        val expanded = adaptCase(token, resolveVariables(macro.expansion))
        val expandedWithTrigger = expanded + trigger

        ic.beginBatchEdit()
        try {
            if (composingLength > 0) {
                ic.finishComposingText()
            }
            val deleted = ic.deleteSurroundingText(token.length, 0)
            if (!deleted) return false
            ic.commitText(expandedWithTrigger, 1)
            lastExpansion = (token + trigger) to expandedWithTrigger
        } finally {
            ic.endBatchEdit()
        }
        return true
    }

    fun findMacro(token: String): Macro? = macros.firstOrNull { m ->
        m.enabled && (if (m.caseSensitive) m.shortcut == token else m.shortcut.equals(token, ignoreCase = true))
    }

    fun lookupPreview(token: String): String? {
        if (token.isEmpty()) return null
        val macro = findMacro(token) ?: return null
        return adaptCase(token, resolveVariables(macro.expansion))
    }

    private fun adaptCase(inputToken: String, expansion: String): String {
        if (expansion.isEmpty()) return expansion
        if (inputToken.length >= 2 && inputToken == inputToken.uppercase() && inputToken != inputToken.lowercase()) {
            return expansion.uppercase()
        }
        if (inputToken.first().isUpperCase()) {
            return expansion.replaceFirstChar { it.uppercaseChar() }
        }
        return expansion
    }

    private fun resolveVariables(text: String): String {
        val now = java.time.ZonedDateTime.now()
        return text
            .replace("{date}", now.toLocalDate().toString())
            .replace("{time}", now.toLocalTime().withNano(0).toString())
    }

    private fun isTechnicalDelimiter(c: Char): Boolean = c in "/@_-#\\=$~`"

    companion object {
        private const val MAX_LOOKBACK = 64
    }
}
===== END FILE: app/src/main/java/com/vnchflex/vietkeyboard/macro/MacroEngine.kt =====

===== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/vnchflex/vietkeyboard/macro/MacroStore.kt =====
package com.vnchflex.vietkeyboard.macro

import android.content.Context
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject

/**
 * Persistent macro store backed by SharedPreferences, preloaded with vnchKey default macros.
 * Caches in memory to avoid JSON parsing on hot paths.
 */
class MacroStore(context: Context) {
    private val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
    private var cache: List<Macro>? = null

    fun load(): List<Macro> {
        val raw = prefs.getString(KEY_MACROS, null)
        val loaded = if (raw == null) defaultMacros() else runCatching { parse(raw) }.getOrElse { defaultMacros() }
        cache = loaded
        return loaded
    }

    fun save(macros: List<Macro>) {
        cache = macros.toList()
        prefs.edit().putString(KEY_MACROS, serialize(macros)).apply()
    }

    fun resetToDefaults(): List<Macro> {
        val defaults = defaultMacros()
        save(defaults)
        return defaults
    }

    fun exportJson(): String {
        val obj = JSONObject()
        load().forEach { m -> obj.put(m.shortcut, m.expansion) }
        return obj.toString(2)
    }

    /** Full backup format preserving caseSensitive/enabled. */
    fun exportBackupJson(): String = serialize(load())

    fun importJson(rawJson: String): Int = importJson(rawJson, replace = false)

    fun importJson(rawJson: String, replace: Boolean): Int {
        val current = if (replace) mutableListOf() else load().toMutableList()
        var importedCount = 0
        val trimmed = rawJson.trim()
        if (trimmed.startsWith("{")) {
            val obj = JSONObject(trimmed)
            val keys = obj.keys()
            while (keys.hasNext()) {
                val k = keys.next().trim().lowercase()
                val v = obj.optString(k, "").trim()
                if (k.isNotEmpty() && v.isNotEmpty()) {
                    current.removeAll { it.shortcut.equals(k, ignoreCase = true) }
                    current.add(Macro(k, v))
                    importedCount++
                }
            }
        } else if (trimmed.startsWith("[")) {
            val parsed = parse(trimmed)
            parsed.forEach { m ->
                current.removeAll { it.shortcut.equals(m.shortcut, ignoreCase = true) }
                current.add(m)
                importedCount++
            }
        }
        if (importedCount > 0 || replace) save(current)
        return importedCount
    }

    private fun parse(raw: String): List<Macro> {
        val arr = JSONArray(raw)
        return List(arr.length()) { i ->
            val o = arr.getJSONObject(i)
            Macro(
                shortcut = o.getString("shortcut"),
                expansion = o.getString("expansion"),
                caseSensitive = o.optBoolean("caseSensitive", false),
                enabled = o.optBoolean("enabled", true),
            )
        }
    }

    private fun serialize(macros: List<Macro>): String {
        val arr = JSONArray()
        macros.forEach { m ->
            arr.put(JSONObject().apply {
                put("shortcut", m.shortcut)
                put("expansion", m.expansion)
                put("caseSensitive", m.caseSensitive)
                put("enabled", m.enabled)
            })
        }
        return arr.toString()
    }

    fun defaultMacros(): List<Macro> = listOf(
        Macro("sg", "Sài Gòn"),
        Macro("hn", "Hà Nội"),
        Macro("dn", "Đồng Nai"),
        Macro("hp", "hạnh phúc"),
        Macro("ct", "Cần Thơ"),
        Macro("cx", "cảm xúc"),
        Macro("dl", "Đà Lạt"),
        Macro("vt", "Vũng Tàu"),
        Macro("bd", "Bình Dương"),
        Macro("ntr", "Nha Trang"),
        Macro("dc", "được"),
        Macro("ko", "không"),
        Macro("vs", "với"),
        Macro("ng", "người"),
        Macro("ch", "chưa"),
        Macro("nt", "nhắn tin"),
        Macro("tn", "tin nhắn"),
        Macro("ntn", "như thế nào"),
        Macro("kb", "không biết"),
        Macro("bh", "bây giờ"),
        Macro("tca", "tất cả"),
        Macro("tica", "tình cảm"),
        Macro("gd", "gia đình"),
        Macro("tvi", "Tiếng Việt"),
        Macro("cty", "công ty"),
        Macro("sđt", "số điện thoại"),
        Macro("stk", "số tài khoản"),
        Macro("tk", "tài khoản"),
        Macro("kh", "khách hàng"),
        Macro("sp", "sản phẩm"),
        Macro("dv", "dịch vụ"),
        Macro("tg", "thời gian"),
        Macro("tt", "thông tin"),
        Macro("lh", "liên hệ"),
        Macro("kn", "kinh nghiệm"),
        Macro("pt", "phát triển"),
        Macro("nv", "nhân viên"),
        Macro("ql", "quản lý"),
        Macro("email", "name@gmail.com"),
        Macro("shrug", "¯\\_(ツ)_/¯"),
    )

    private companion object {
        const val PREFS_NAME = "viet_keyboard_macros"
        const val KEY_MACROS = "macros_v2"
    }
}

    /** Bỏ dấu tự do theo phong cách vnchKey (cho phép gõ dấu ở cuối từ sau phụ âm cuối hoặc ngay sau nguyên âm) */
    var freeTonePlacement: Boolean

        set(v) = prefs.edit().putBoolean("pref_vn_free_tone", v).apply()

        set(v) = prefs.edit().putBoolean("pref_vn_quick_telex", v).apply()

    /** Tốc gõ phụ âm cuối (-g -> -ng, -h -> -nh, -k -> -c) */

        get() = prefs.getBoolean("pref_vn_relaxed_coda", false)
        set(v) = prefs.edit().putBoolean("pref_vn_relaxed_coda", v).apply()

    /** Phương ngữ Nam Bộ & Phụ âm mở rộng (dz/f/j/w/z: dzìa, dzách, dzô, zậy, fải, wá...) */

        get() = prefs.getBoolean("pref_vn_southern_genz", true)
        set(v) = prefs.edit().putBoolean("pref_vn_southern_genz", v).apply()

    /** Bật Macro / Bảng gõ tắt (vnchKey) */
    var macroEnabled: Boolean
        get() = prefs.getBoolean("pref_macro_enabled", true)
        set(v) = prefs.edit().putBoolean("pref_macro_enabled", v).apply()

    /** Danh sách từ ngoại lệ (whitelist) do người dùng thêm để không chuyển sang Tiếng Việt */
    var englishWhitelist: Set<String>
        get() = prefs.getStringSet("pref_english_whitelist", emptySet())?.toSet() ?: emptySet()
        set(v) = prefs.edit().putStringSet(
            "pref_english_whitelist",
            v.map { it.trim().lowercase() }.filter { it.isNotEmpty() }.toSet()
        ).apply()

    fun keyHeightScale(): Float = when (keyboardHeight) {
        "extra_short" -> 0.82f
        "short" -> 0.90f
        "mid_short" -> 0.95f
        "normal" -> 1.00f
        "mid_tall" -> 1.06f
        "tall" -> 1.12f
        "extra_tall" -> 1.20f
        else -> 1.00f
    }

    companion object {
        const val PREFS_NAME = "vnchflex_gboard_prefs"
    }
}
